      function renderBaseActions(viewModel = null) {
        const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
        let box = $("#actionButtons"),
          t = panelView.actorTeam,
          s = panelView.actorSlot,
          u = panelView.actor;
        const inputProjection = panelView.projection;
        box.innerHTML = "";
        let dual =
          cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") && game.dualActorController?.uid === u.uid
            ? ` · ${game.dualActorController.actor === "beast" ? "빙마 행동" : "기수 행동"}`
            : "";
        $("#actionTitle").textContent = game.winner
          ? "전투 종료"
          : busy
            ? (game?.mode === "online"
                ? (onlineAutoCommandActive
                    ? "예약 행동 처리 중…"
                    : (typeof onlinePendingCommand !== "undefined" && onlinePendingCommand
                        ? "행동 동기화 중…"
                        : "온라인 처리 중…"))
                : "AI 계산 중…")
          : inputProjection.ownsCurrentActor
              ? u && !u.dead
                ? `${u.name}${dual}`
                : `슬롯 ${s + 1} 출전`
              : game.mode === "online"
                ? "상대 행동 대기 중…"
                : "자동 진행 중";
        $("#actionHint").textContent = pendingSkill
          ? "유효한 대상을 선택하세요."
          : game.fieldEffect?.type === "grand"
            ? `그랜드필드: ${game.fieldEffect.team === "P" ? "플레이어" : "AI"} 진영 소유`
            : "";
        if (game.winner) {
          box.innerHTML =
            '<button id="sameRestart" data-action-panel-meta="terminal">같은 조건으로 재시작</button><button data-nav="setup" data-action-panel-meta="terminal">메인 메뉴</button>';
          $("#sameRestart").onclick = () => startBattle(game.seed);
          box.querySelector("[data-nav]").onclick = () => NavigationApplication.request("setup");
          return;
        }
        let controllable = inputProjection.ownsCurrentActor,
          acts = inputProjection.actions;
        if (!acts.length) {
          $("#actionHint").textContent = "행동 불가 · 자동으로 턴을 넘깁니다.";
          return;
        }
        if (busy || !controllable || !inputProjection.canAct) return;
        if (!u || u.dead) {
          acts.forEach((a) =>
            actionButton(box, a.label, () => {
              const executed = execute(game, t, s, a);
              if (executed) afterAction();
            }),
          );
          return;
        }
        let speedDelay = acts.find((a) => a.type === "speedDelay");
        if (speedDelay) {
          let token = game.token,
            serial = game.turnSerial;
          busy = true;
          renderActions();
          scheduleLocalAutoTurnTimer(
            "speed-delay",
            true ? 550 : 0,
            () => {
              if (
                !game ||
                game.winner ||
                game.token !== token ||
                game.turnSerial !== serial
              ) {
                busy = false;
                return;
              }
              const executed = execute(game, t, s, speedDelay);
              busy = false;
              if (executed) afterAction();
            },
          );
          return;
        }
        let scheduled = acts.find((a) => a.type === "scheduled");

            $("#actionTitle").textContent = p2p?.role === "host"

      const ActionRenderEngine = (() => {
        const routes = new Map(), decorators = new Map();
        let renderSerial = 0, lastRender = null;
        const ordered = map => [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        const registerRoute = (id,priority,when,render) => {
          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});
          return ()=>routes.delete(String(id));
        };
        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {
          if(!id||typeof decorate!=="function"||typeof when!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render decorator");
          decorators.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,decorate,when});
          return ()=>decorators.delete(String(id));
        };
        const context = viewModel => {
          const pair=game?.order?.[game.turn]||[];
          return {game,team:pair[0],slot:pair[1],unit:pair.length?unitAt(game,pair[0],pair[1]):null,box:document.getElementById("actionButtons"),viewModel};
        };
        const render = (viewModel = null, requestReason = "unspecified") => {
          const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          let ctx=context(panelView),result;
          const route=ordered(routes).find(entry=>entry.when(ctx));
          result=route?route.render(ctx):renderBaseActions(panelView);
          ctx=context(panelView);
          for(const entry of ordered(decorators))if(entry.when(ctx))entry.decorate(ctx,result);
          const projection=panelView.projection;
          const buttons=[...(ctx.box?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          const fingerprint=actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|");
          lastRender=Object.freeze({
            renderSerial:++renderSerial,
            renderedAt:Date.now(),
            requestReason:String(requestReason||"unspecified"),
            routeId:route?.id||"base",
            battleId:panelView.battleId,
            sessionGeneration:Number(window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.().generation||0),
            networkRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeRevision:Number(projection.authoritativeRevision||0),
            actionPanelViewModelRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            renderedDomRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            viewModelSignature:panelView.signature,
            turnSerial:panelView.turnSerial,
            actorUid:projection.currentActorUid||null,
            actorCanonicalTeam:projection.currentActorCanonicalTeam||null,
            localCanonicalTeam:projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null,
            canAct:projection.canAct,
            reasons:[...projection.reasons],
            legalActionCount:Number(projection.legalActionCount||0),
            manualLegalActionCount:Number(projection.manualLegalActionCount||0),
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            buttonFingerprint:fingerprint,
            gatherRendered:actionButtons.some(button=>button.textContent?.includes("에너지 모으기")),
          });
          return result;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          registerRoute,registerDecorator,render,base:()=>ActionPanelConvergenceOwner.request("action-render-base-api",{force:true}),
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            renderSerial=0; lastRender=null;
            const box=document.getElementById("actionButtons"); if(box)box.innerHTML="";
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),renderSerial,lastRender:null});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({
            routes:ordered(routes).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),

            renderSerial,
            lastRender:lastRender?{...lastRender,reasons:[...lastRender.reasons]}:null,

        let requestSerial=0,commitSerial=0,skippedDuplicateCount=0,lastRequest=null;
        const domState = () => {
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons");
          const buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          return Object.freeze({
            rootConnected:!!root?.isConnected,
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledActionButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            fingerprint:actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|"),
          });
        };
        const mustBeEmpty = view => {
          const reasons=view.projection.reasons||[];
          return view.terminal || reasons.some(reason=>[
            "REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN","SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY","AI_CONTROLLED_MODE","PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION",
            "PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION","APPLICATION_BUSY","TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED","BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED",
            "RECONNECTING","CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK","ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK"
          ].includes(reason));
        };
        const matches = (view,renderState=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom=domState()) => {
          const prior=renderState.lastRender;
          if(!prior||prior.viewModelSignature!==view.signature)return false;
          if(Number(prior.actionPanelViewModelRevision||0)!==Number(view.canonicalRevision||0))return false;
          if(Number(prior.renderedDomRevision||0)!==Number(view.canonicalRevision||0))return false;
          if(prior.actorUid!==(view.projection.currentActorUid||null))return false;

          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons"),buttons=[...(root?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];

          const state=domState();lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId:++requestSerial,reason:String(reason),committed:true,coherent:true,suspended:true,signature:null,canonicalRevision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0),turnSerial:Number(game?.turnSerial||0),actorUid:null,dom:state,at:Date.now()});
          return lastRequest;
        };
        const request = (reason="action-panel-convergence", options={}) => {
          const view=ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons");if(root)delete root.dataset.actionPanelSuspended;
          const before=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),beforeDom=domState();
          const coherent=!options.force&&matches(view,before,beforeDom);
          const requestId=++requestSerial;
          if(coherent){
            skippedDuplicateCount++;
            lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId,reason:String(reason),committed:false,coherent:true,signature:view.signature,canonicalRevision:view.canonicalRevision,turnSerial:view.turnSerial,actorUid:view.projection.currentActorUid||null,dom:beforeDom,at:Date.now()});

          const after=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),afterDom=domState();
          const converged=matches(view,after,afterDom);
          lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId,reason:String(reason),committed:true,coherent:converged,signature:view.signature,canonicalRevision:view.canonicalRevision,turnSerial:view.turnSerial,actorUid:view.projection.currentActorUid||null,dom:afterDom,at:Date.now()});

          return lastRequest;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          request,suspend,
          matches,
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            requestSerial=0; commitSerial=0; skippedDuplicateCount=0; lastRequest=null;
            ActionRenderEngine.reset(reason);
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),requestSerial,commitSerial,lastRequest:null,render:ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom:{...domState()}});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({requestSerial,commitSerial,skippedDuplicateCount,lastRequest:lastRequest?{...lastRequest,dom:{...lastRequest.dom}}:null,render:ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom:{...domState()}}),
        });

      function chooseSummonAIAction(g,t,u){
        const opts=summonActionOptions(g,t,u).filter(a=>a.type==='summonSkill');
        let pick=null;
        if(cardCapability(u,'summonBehavior','')==='support'){
          const injured=summonTargetRefs(g,t,true).map(x=>x.u).filter(x=>x.hp<x.maxHp).sort((a,b)=>(b.maxHp-b.hp)-(a.maxHp-a.hp))[0];
          const enemy=summonTargetRefs(g,t,false).map(x=>x.u).filter(x=>allEnergy(x)>.001).sort((a,b)=>allEnergy(b)-allEnergy(a))[0];
          if(enemy)pick=opts.find(a=>actionSkillHasRuntimeKind(a, "summonEnergyPeck")&&a.targetUid===enemy.uid);
          if(!pick&&injured)pick=opts.find(a=>actionSkillHasRuntimeKind(a, "summonHeal")&&a.targetUid===injured.uid);
        }else{
          const enemy=bestSummonEnemy(g,t);
          pick=enemy&&opts.find(a=>a.targetUid===enemy.uid);
        }
        if(!pick)return {type:'summonGather'};
        const cost=summonSkillCost(g,t,u,pick.skill),plan=cost?findPaymentPlan(g,t,u,cost):{R:0,W:0,B:0};
        return {...pick,paymentPlan:plan};
      }

      function rawReqFromCost(cost) {
        const tokens = costTokens(cost), req = { R:0, W:0, B:0, any:0, wb:0 };
        for (const x of tokens) {
          if (x === "*") req.any++;
          else if (x === "WorB") req.wb++;
          else req[x]++;
        }
        return { req, tokens, text: tokens.join("") };
      }

      function dmLocalTurn(g, t) {
        if (g.mode === "human") return t === "P";
        if (g.mode === "online") return t === onlineSide;
        return false;
      }
      function dmChooseMemoryOption(option) {
        if (!game) return;
        const [t,s] = game.order[game.turn], actor = unitAt(game,t,s);
        if (!actor || actor.memoryUsed || !copiedSkillAllowed(actor,option.skill)) return;
        const variants = (option.costVariants || [option.cost]).filter(cost => canPayAction(game,t,actor,cost));
        if (!variants.length) return;
        const actionId = dmNewActionId(game,actor);
        const targets = dmTargetRefs(game,t,s,actor,option.skill);
        const actions = [];
        for (const cost of variants) for (const target of targets) actions.push(dmMemoryAction(game,t,s,actor,option,cost,target,actionId));
        if (!actions.length) { TargetSelectionApplication.cancel("dragon-memory-actions-empty", { dragonMemory:true, releaseBusy:true, closeModal:true }); return; }
        dmPendingFlow = {actionId,actorUid:actor.uid,sourceUid:option.unit.uid,skillId:option.skill.id,stage:"target-or-payment"};
        const uniqueTargets = [];
        const seen = new Set();
        for (const a of actions) {
          const key = `${a.target?.team}:${a.target?.slot}:${a.target?.uid || "slot"}`;
          if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); uniqueTargets.push(a.target); }
        }
        const noChoice = ["self","field"].includes(option.skill.target) || uniqueTargets.length === 1;
        $("#modal").classList.remove("open");
        if (noChoice) {
          dmPendingFlow.stage = "payment";
          dispatchPlayerSkill(actions);
          return;
        }
        dmPendingFlow.stage = "target";
        TargetSelectionApplication.begin({
          actions, targets:uniqueTargets, slotTarget:option.skill.target === "enemySlot", reason:"dragon-memory-multicost-target-selection"
        });
      }

      /* UI에서는 복사 후보 액션을 직접 노출하지 않고 0코스트 진입 버튼 하나만 둔다. */
      function decorateDragonMemoryActions(viewModel=null) {
        if (!game || !viewModel?.projection?.canAct) return;
        const box = $("#actionButtons"), [t, s] = game.order[game.turn], actor = unitAt(game, t, s);
        if (!box || !actor) return;
        [...box.querySelectorAll("button")].forEach(button => {
          if ((button.textContent || "").trim().startsWith("드래곤메모리")) button.remove();
        });
        if (dmPendingFlow?.stage === "target" && pendingSkill?.actions?.some(a => actionSkillHasTag(a, "copy"))) {
          box.innerHTML = "";
          const cancel = document.createElement("button");
          cancel.className = "dragon-memory-target-cancel";
          cancel.textContent = "대상 선택 취소";
          cancel.onclick = () => TargetSelectionApplication.cancel("dragon-memory-target-cancel", { dragonMemory:true, releaseBusy:true, closeModal:true });
          box.appendChild(cancel);
          return;
        }
        if (!!firstSkillByTag(actor, "copy") && !actor.dead && !actor.skillSeals?.[skillIdByTag(actor, "copy")] && !status(actor, "freeze") && !status(actor, "skillSeal") && dmLocalTurn(game, t) && memoryOptions(game, t, actor).length) {
          const button = document.createElement("button");
          button.className = "skillbtn dragon-memory-main-button";
          button.innerHTML = `<b>드래곤메모리</b> <span class="cost">0</span><br><span class="tiny">후열에 있는 드래곤의 기술을 복사한다.</span>`;
          button.onclick = () => { if (!busy && !dmPendingFlow) openDragonMemoryMenu(); };
          box.appendChild(button);
        }
      }

      /* R49 generic reactive-energy trait lifecycle.
         A card opts in only through card.reactiveEnergyMaxHp; mutation owners never know card ids. */
      function allReactiveEnergyUnits(g) {
        if (!g?.teams) return [];
        const seen = new Set(), out = [];
        for (const t of ["P", "A"])
          for (const u of [...(g.teams[t].field || []), ...(g.teams[t].bench || []), ...(g.teams[t].graveyard || [])])
            if (u && !seen.has(u.uid)) { seen.add(u.uid); out.push(u); }
        return out;
      }
      function reactiveEnergyRule(u) {
        const rule = cardCapability(u, "reactiveEnergyMaxHp", null);
        return rule && typeof rule === "object" ? rule : null;
      }
      function reactiveEnergyObservedKey(u, rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u)) {
        if (!rule) return null;
        return String(rule.observedStateKey || `_reactiveEnergyObserved_${String(rule.color || "any")}`);
      }
      function reactiveEnergyAmount(u, rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u)) {
        if (!u || !rule) return 0;
        const color = String(rule.color || "");
        return +(energyStore(u).reduce((n, e) => n + (!color || e.c === color ? Number(e.a || 0) : 0), 0)).toFixed(3);
      }
      function initReactiveEnergyUnit(u) {
        const rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u);
        if (!u || !rule) return u;
        const key = reactiveEnergyObservedKey(u, rule);
        if (!Number.isFinite(Number(u[key]))) u[key] = reactiveEnergyAmount(u, rule);
        return u;
      }

      function playGenociderVisual(v) {
        const actor = battleEntityElement(v?.uid);
        const target = v?.targetUid ? battleEntityElement(v.targetUid) : actor;
        const arena = document.querySelector('.arena');
        const placeNode = (node, point) => { if (!node || !point) return; node.style.left = `${point.x}px`; node.style.top = `${point.y}px`; };
        if (v?.type === "genociderAbsorbFx") {
          if (!actor) return;
          const node = createGenoSiphonParticles(actor, Math.max(.5, Number(v.amount || 0)), "geno-devour-absorb");
          window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => node?.remove(), 900);
          visualHoldMs = Math.max(visualHoldMs, 900);
          return;
        }
        if (v?.type === "genociderClawFx") {
          if (!target) return;
          const show = () => {
            const node = document.createElement("div");
            node.className = "geno-claw-fx";
            node.style.setProperty("--claw-tilt", v.hit === 2 ? "15deg" : "-22deg");
            for (const [y, shift] of [["16%", -2], ["47%", 8], ["79%", -6]]) {
              const line = document.createElement("i");
              line.className = "geno-claw-streak";
              line.style.setProperty("--claw-y", y);
              line.style.marginLeft = `${shift}px`;
              node.appendChild(line);
            }
            target.appendChild(node);
            window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => node.remove(), 760);
          };
          window.TimerManager.setTimeout(show, v.hit === 2 ? 170 : 0);
          visualHoldMs = Math.max(visualHoldMs, 1120);
          return;
        }
        if (v?.type === "genociderExplosionFx") {
          const power = Math.max(0, Number(v.power || 0));
          const actual = Math.max(0, combatInt(v.actualAmount ?? v.amount ?? 0));
          const actorPoint = arena ? pointForUid(arena, v.uid) : null;
          const targetPoint = arena ? pointForUid(arena, v.targetUid || v.uid) : null;
          const siphonLife = Math.min(1500, 760 + power * 180);
          const vortexLife = Math.min(2200, 980 + power * 280);
          const impactLife = Math.min(1700, 940 + power * 220);
          const totalLife = Math.min(3000, 1380 + power * 520);
          if (actor && actorPoint && arena) {
            const siphon = createGenoSiphonParticles(actor, power + 1.1, "geno-displosion-siphon");
            window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => siphon?.remove(), siphonLife);
            window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => {
              if (!arena.isConnected || !actorPoint) return;
              const vortex = document.createElement("div");
              vortex.className = "geno-displosion-vortex";
              vortex.style.setProperty("--geno-power", String(power));
              vortex.style.setProperty("--v-time", `${Math.min(1.95, 1.05 + power * .28)}s`);
              placeNode(vortex, actorPoint);
              arena.appendChild(vortex);
              window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => vortex.remove(), vortexLife);
            }, 180);
          }
          window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => {
            if (!arena || !targetPoint) return;
            const impact = document.createElement("div");
            impact.className = "geno-displosion-impact";
            impact.style.setProperty("--geno-power", String(power));
            impact.style.setProperty("--i-time", `${Math.min(1.55, .92 + power * .2)}s`);
            placeNode(impact, targetPoint);
            const shardCount = 12 + Math.round(power * 8);
            for (let i = 0; i < shardCount; i++) {
              const shard = document.createElement("i");
              shard.className = "geno-impact-shard";
              shard.style.setProperty("--geno-power", String(power));
              shard.style.setProperty("--shard-angle", `${Math.round(i * 360 / shardCount)}deg`);
              shard.style.setProperty("--s-time", `${Math.min(1.15, .76 + power * .08)}s`);
              impact.appendChild(shard);
            }
            arena.appendChild(impact);
            window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => impact.remove(), impactLife);
          }, Math.min(1600, 520 + power * 260));
          visualHoldMs = Math.max(visualHoldMs, totalLife);
        }
      }

      function timeClockMiniHTMLCore(u,g) {
        const team=timeTeamOf(g,u), local=localTeamFor(g), theme=team===local?"gold":"blue", aligned=timeIsAligned(u);
        const stopReason=timeClockStopReason(g,u), stopped=!!stopReason, cancelled=aligned&&timeClockAlignmentsCancel(g);
        const state=stopped
          ? (stopReason==="traitSeal"?"정지 · 특성봉인":"정지 · 상대 시계 정렬")
          : cancelled?"동시 정렬 · 효과 상쇄":aligned?"정렬 · 자신 외 전원 특성 정지":"비정렬";
        const stateClass=stopped?"stopped":cancelled?"cancelled":aligned?"aligned":"";
        return `<div class="time-mini-clock-wrap ${stopped?"clock-stopped-wrap":""}"><div class="time-mini-clock ${theme}${stopped?" clock-stopped":""}${cancelled?" clock-cancelled":""}"><span class="time-clock-hand hour" style="transform:translateX(-50%) rotate(${timeNormDeg(u.timeHourDeg)}deg)"></span><span class="time-clock-hand minute" style="transform:translateX(-50%) rotate(${timeNormDeg(u.timeMinuteDeg)}deg)"></span><span class="time-clock-pin"></span><span class="time-clock-word">Time</span></div><div><b>Time</b><br><span>시침 ${timeNormDeg(u.timeHourDeg)}° · 분침 ${timeNormDeg(u.timeMinuteDeg)}°</span><br><span class="time-mini-state ${stateClass}">${state}</span></div></div>`;
      }
      function timeClockMiniHTML(...args) { return invokeBossModeHook("timeClockMiniHTML",timeClockMiniHTMLCore,this,args); }

      function timeSuppressedUidSet(g) {
        const domain = g ? timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g) : null;
        return new Set([...(domain?.suppressedUids || [])].map(String));
      }
      function timeUpdateSuppressedTeamAttribute(g, uids = timeSuppressedUidSet(g)) {
        const teams = new Set();
        if (g && uids.size) for (const team of ["P", "A"]) {
          for (const unit of g.teams?.[team]?.field || []) {
            if (unit?.uid != null && !unit.dead && uids.has(String(unit.uid))) teams.add(team);
          }
        }
        if (teams.size === 1) document.body.dataset.fateClockSuppressedTeam = [...teams][0];
        else delete document.body.dataset.fateClockSuppressedTeam;
        return teams;
      }
      function timeSyncSuppressionVisuals(g = (typeof game !== "undefined" ? game : null)) {
        const domain = g ? timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g) : null;
        const uids = timeSuppressedUidSet(g);
        const cancelled = !!g && timeClockAlignmentsCancel(g);
        timeUpdateSuppressedTeamAttribute(g, uids);
        document.querySelectorAll('.arena .battle-unit-card[data-zone="field"],.arena .summon-card').forEach(el => {
          const uid = el.dataset.uid || el.dataset.entityId;
          const unit = g && uid != null ? findUnit(g, uid) : null;
          const suppressed = uid != null && uids.has(String(uid));
          const stopped = cardHasCapability(unit, "timeControl") && !!timeClockStopReason(g, unit);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-trait-suppressed', suppressed);
          el.classList.toggle('fate-clock-trait-blocked', suppressed);
          el.toggleAttribute('data-fate-clock-trait-blocked', suppressed);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-stopped-owner', stopped);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-owner-active', !!domain && uid != null && domain.owns(uid) && !suppressed);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-owner-cancelled', cancelled && cardHasCapability(unit, "timeControl") && timeIsAligned(unit) && !stopped);
        });
        return uids;
      }
            RenderLayerOwner.add({
        enter(){
          const current=typeof game!=="undefined"?game:null;
          timeUpdateSuppressedTeamAttribute(current,timeSuppressedUidSet(current));
          const old=timeTraitGame;timeTraitGame=current;return {old,current};
        },
        leave(token){timeTraitGame=token?.old??null;},
        transform(result,token){timeSyncSuppressionVisuals(token?.current||null);return result;}
      });

      function timeClockFaceNode(theme,beforeHour,beforeMinute) {
        const face=document.createElement('div'); face.className=`time-clock-face ${theme}`;
        face.innerHTML='<span class="time-clock-tick t0"></span><span class="time-clock-tick t1"></span><span class="time-clock-tick t2"></span><span class="time-clock-tick t3"></span><span class="time-clock-hand hour"></span><span class="time-clock-hand minute"></span><span class="time-clock-pin"></span><span class="time-clock-word">Time</span>';
        const h=face.querySelector('.hour'),m=face.querySelector('.minute');
        h.style.transform=`translateX(-50%) rotate(${beforeHour}deg)`;m.style.transform=`translateX(-50%) rotate(${beforeMinute}deg)`;
        return face;
      }
      function timeAnimateHand(el,from,to,duration,extraTurns=0) {
        const delta=((to-from)%360+360)%360;
        const destination=from+delta+Math.max(0,Number(extraTurns)||0)*360;
        return el.animate([{transform:`translateX(-50%) rotate(${from}deg)`},{transform:`translateX(-50%) rotate(${destination}deg)`}],{duration,easing:'cubic-bezier(.12,.82,.14,1)',fill:'forwards'}).finished.catch(()=>{});
      }

          {transform:`translateX(-50%) rotate(${base+16}deg)`},

          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});

        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {

      /* R49 generic reactive-energy trait lifecycle.
         A card opts in only through card.reactiveEnergyMaxHp; mutation owners never know card ids. */
      function allReactiveEnergyUnits(g) {
        if (!g?.teams) return [];
        const seen = new Set(), out = [];
        for (const t of ["P", "A"])
          for (const u of [...(g.teams[t].field || []), ...(g.teams[t].bench || []), ...(g.teams[t].graveyard || [])])
            if (u && !seen.has(u.uid)) { seen.add(u.uid); out.push(u); }
        return out;
      }
      function reactiveEnergyRule(u) {
        const rule = cardCapability(u, "reactiveEnergyMaxHp", null);
        return rule && typeof rule === "object" ? rule : null;
      }
      function reactiveEnergyObservedKey(u, rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u)) {
        if (!rule) return null;
        return String(rule.observedStateKey || `_reactiveEnergyObserved_${String(rule.color || "any")}`);
      }
      function reactiveEnergyAmount(u, rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u)) {
        if (!u || !rule) return 0;
        const color = String(rule.color || "");
        return +(energyStore(u).reduce((n, e) => n + (!color || e.c === color ? Number(e.a || 0) : 0), 0)).toFixed(3);
      }
      function initReactiveEnergyUnit(u) {
        const rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u);
        if (!u || !rule) return u;
        const key = reactiveEnergyObservedKey(u, rule);
        if (!Number.isFinite(Number(u[key]))) u[key] = reactiveEnergyAmount(u, rule);
        return u;
      }
      function syncReactiveEnergyMaxHp(g, u, reason = "", showNotice = true) {
        const rule = reactiveEnergyRule(u);
        if (!u || !rule) return 0;
        initReactiveEnergyUnit(u);
        const baseMaxHp = Math.max(0, Number(rule.baseMaxHp ?? card(u).maxHp ?? u.maxHp ?? 0));
        const oldMax = Math.max(0, Number(u.maxHp || baseMaxHp));
        const resource = traitsActive(u) ? reactiveEnergyAmount(u, rule) : 0;
        const nextMax = combatInt(baseMaxHp + resource * Number(rule.maxHpPer || 0));
        u.maxHp = Math.max(baseMaxHp, nextMax);
        if (u.hp > u.maxHp) {
          u.hp = u.maxHp;
          if (g) log(g, `${u.name}: 최대 HP 변화로 현재 HP가 ${u.hp}/${u.maxHp}로 조정됐다.`);
        }
        if (showNotice && g && oldMax !== u.maxHp) {
          const delta = u.maxHp - oldMax;
          queueCombatNotice(g, u, `최대 HP ${delta > 0 ? "+" : ""}${delta}`, "effect");
        }
        return u.maxHp - oldMax;
      }

            target.appendChild(node);
            window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => node.remove(), 760);
          };
          window.TimerManager.setTimeout(show, v.hit === 2 ? 170 : 0);
          visualHoldMs = Math.max(visualHoldMs, 1120);
          return;
        }
        if (v?.type === "genociderExplosionFx") {

      function timeClockMiniHTMLCore(u,g) {

      function timeSuppressedUidSet(g) {
        const domain = g ? timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g) : null;
        return new Set([...(domain?.suppressedUids || [])].map(String));
      }
      function timeUpdateSuppressedTeamAttribute(g, uids = timeSuppressedUidSet(g)) {
        const teams = new Set();
        if (g && uids.size) for (const team of ["P", "A"]) {
          for (const unit of g.teams?.[team]?.field || []) {
            if (unit?.uid != null && !unit.dead && uids.has(String(unit.uid))) teams.add(team);
          }
        }
        if (teams.size === 1) document.body.dataset.fateClockSuppressedTeam = [...teams][0];
        else delete document.body.dataset.fateClockSuppressedTeam;
        return teams;
      }
      function timeSyncSuppressionVisuals(g = (typeof game !== "undefined" ? game : null)) {
        const domain = g ? timeCurrentSuppressionDomain(g) : null;
        const uids = timeSuppressedUidSet(g);
        const cancelled = !!g && timeClockAlignmentsCancel(g);
        timeUpdateSuppressedTeamAttribute(g, uids);
        document.querySelectorAll('.arena .battle-unit-card[data-zone="field"],.arena .summon-card').forEach(el => {

          el.classList.toggle('clock-trait-suppressed', suppressed);
          el.classList.toggle('fate-clock-trait-blocked', suppressed);
          el.toggleAttribute('data-fate-clock-trait-blocked', suppressed);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-stopped-owner', stopped);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-owner-active', !!domain && uid != null && domain.owns(uid) && !suppressed);
          el.classList.toggle('clock-owner-cancelled', cancelled && cardHasCapability(unit, "timeControl") && timeIsAligned(unit) && !stopped);
        });
        return uids;
      }

      function timeAnimateHand(el,from,to,duration,extraTurns=0) {
        const delta=((to-from)%360+360)%360;
        const destination=from+delta+Math.max(0,Number(extraTurns)||0)*360;
        return el.animate([{transform:`translateX(-50%) rotate(${from}deg)`},{transform:`translateX(-50%) rotate(${destination}deg)`}],{duration,easing:'cubic-bezier(.12,.82,.14,1)',fill:'forwards'}).finished.catch(()=>{});
      }

    interceptGrant(g,u,color,amount=1,specs=null,actor=null){

    clockVisualDuplicate(events){

,selection);
      return {handled:true,value:applied==null?false:applied};
    },
    interceptGrant(g,u,color,amount=1,specs=null,actor=null){
      const max=card(u).maxEnergy||4;
      if(overflowRuntime&&!forcedByOpponent(g,u)&&totalEnergy(u,actor)+amount>max+.001)
        return {handled:true,value:gainEnergy(g,u,color,amount,null,actor)};
      return {handled:false};
    },
    interceptFinalize(action){
      const [t]=game?.order?.[game.turn]||[];
      if(!action||action.energyOverflowResolved||!playerControlsTeam(game,t)||actionSkillHasEffect(action,"eternaHeal"))return {handled:false};
      const event=auditNextOverflow(action,[]);
      if(!event)return {handled:false};
      return {handled:true,value:openOverflowChoice(action,[],event,finalizeChosenPlayerAction)};
    },
    clockVisualDuplicate(events){
      const signature=clockVisualSignature(events),now=Date.now();
      for(const [key,at] of playedClockVisuals)if(now-at>12000)playedClockVisuals.delete(key);
      if(signature&&!signature.endsWith(":")&&playedClockVisuals.has(signature))return true;
      if(signature&&!signature.endsWith(":"))playedClockVisuals.set(signature,now);
      return false;
    },
    resetClockVisualDedup(){playedClockVisuals.clear();}
  };
})();

<script id="userRequestedInteractionFixR25">
(() => {
  "use strict";
  const VERSION = "requested-interaction-fix-r25";
  const countsEqual = (left, right) =>
    ["R","W","B"].every(color =>
      Math.abs(Number(left?.[color] || 0) - Number(right?.[color] || 0)) < .001
    );
  const energyToken = entry => `${entry.c}${Number(entry.a) === .5 ? "½" : ""}`;

  function openAbyssCannonChoiceR25(actions) {
    const [team, slot] = game.order[game.turn];
    const actor = unitAt(game, team, slot);
    if (!actor || !actions?.length) return;
    const part = actions[0].actor || null;
    const store = energyStore(actor, part);
    const picked = new Set();
    const root = document.getElementById("modalContent");
    root.innerHTML = `<h2>초저주포 · 에너지 선택</h2>
      <p>지불할 에너지를 바로 고르세요. 선택한 실제 에너지 수가 곧 코스트가 됩니다.</p>
      <div id="abyssDirectEnergy" class="energy abyss-direct-energy"></div>
      <div id="abyssDirectPreview" class="effect-box"></div>
      <div class="choice-list">
        <button id="abyssDirectConfirm" class="primary">이 에너지로 사용</button>
        <button id="abyssDirectClear">선택 해제</button>
        <button id="abyssDirectCancel">취소</button>
      </div>`;
    const box = root.querySelector("#abyssDirectEnergy");
    const preview = root.querySelector("#abyssDirectPreview");
    const confirm = root.querySelector("#abyssDirectConfirm");
    const selectedCounts = () => {
      const result = {R:0,W:0,B:0};
      for (const index of picked) result[store[index].c] += Number(store[index].a || 0);
      return result;
    };
    const refresh = () => {
      const counts = selectedCounts();
      const total = counts.R + counts.W + counts.B;
      const matching = actions.filter(action =>
        Math.abs(Number(action.baseCostCount || 0) - total) < .001 &&
        countsEqual(action.paymentPlan, counts)
      );
      const chosen = matching[0] || null;
      const poison = counts.R > 0 && counts.W > 0 && counts.B > 0;
      const damage = chosen ? Math.max(0, 100 - 40 * (4 - Number(chosen.baseCostCount || 0))) : 0;
      preview.textContent = chosen
        ? `선택 ${total}E · ${damage}D${poison ? " · 독 부여" : ""}`
        : `선택 ${total}E · 사용할 수 없는 조합입니다. 0~4E의 실제 에너지를 고르세요.`;
      confirm.disabled = !chosen;
      return {chosen, counts};
    };
    store.forEach((entry, index) => {
      const button = document.createElement("button");
      button.type = "button";
      button.className = `e ${entry.c} abyss-energy-chip`;
      button.textContent = energyToken(entry);
      button.onclick = () => {
        picked.has(index) ? picked.delete(index) : picked.add(index);
        button.classList.toggle("chosen", picked.has(index));
        refresh();
      };
      box.appendChild(button);
    });
    if (!store.length) box.innerHTML = `<span class="muted"

      function enemyTargets(g, t) {
        let a = [];
        g.teams[other(t)].field.forEach((x, i) => {
          if (x && !x.dead) a.push({ team: other(t), slot: i, uid: x.uid });
        });
        return a;
      }
      function legalActionsCore(g, t, s) {
        prepareCurrentTurn(g);
        let team = g.teams[t],
          u = team.field[s],
          a = [];
        if (!u || u.dead) {
          team.bench
            .filter((x) => x && !x.dead)
            .forEach((x) =>
              a.push({ type: "deploy", unit: x.uid, label: `${x.name} 출전` }),
            );
          return a;
        }
        if (u.speedDelayTurns > 0)
          return [{ type:"speedDelay", label:`Speed ${card(u).speed} · 출전 지연 자동 진행` }];
        if (u.scheduledAction) {
          const scheduled = ensureScheduledActionIdentity(g, u);
          return [{
            type: "scheduled",
            actionId: scheduledExecutionId(g, scheduled),
            scheduledActionId: scheduled.scheduledActionId,
            sourceInstanceId: scheduled.sourceInstanceId,
            label: "예약 공격 자동 실행",
          }];
        }
        let frozen = status(u, "freeze"),
          sealed = status(u, "skillSeal");
        if (cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor")) {
          let who =
              g.dualActorController?.uid === u.uid
                ? g.dualActorController.actor
                : "beast",
            store = energyStore(u, who);
          if (!status(u, "paralyze"))
            a.push({
              type: "gather",
              actor: who,
              label: `${who === "beast" ? "빙마" : "기수"} 에너지 모으기`,
            });
          a.push({
            type: "dualPass",
            actor: who,
            label: `${who === "beast" ? "빙마" : "기수"} 행동 넘기기`,
          });
          if (who === "beast" && !frozen && canSwitch(g, t, u))
            for (let i = 0; i < team.bench.length; i++)
              if (team.bench[i] && !team.bench[i].dead)
                a.push({
                  type:"switch", bench:i, switchCost:switchCost(u),
                  label:`${team.bench[i].name}와 교체 · ${switchCostText(u)} (${(team.switchUses || 0) + 1}/3)`,
                });
          if (!frozen && !sealed) {
            let skills = card(u).skills.filter((sk) =>
              who === "beast"
                ? !skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance")
                : skillDefHasEffect(sk, "shatterLance"),
            );
            for (const sk of skills) {
              if (!skillRequirementsMet(u, sk) || !canPayFrom(u, sk.cost, who)) continue;
              if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "iceRoar"))
                a.push({
                  type: "skill",
                  skill: sk.id,
                  actor: who,
                  target: { team: t, slot: s, uid: u.uid },
                  label: sk.name,
                });
              else if (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "rampage")) {
                let max = Math.max(0, allEnergy(u) - 1);
                for (const tar of enemyTargets(g, t))
                  for (let extra = 0; extra <= max + 0.001; extra += 0.5)
                    a.push({
                      type: "skill",
                      skill: sk.id,
                      actor: who,
                      target: tar,
                      extra: +extra.toFixed(1),
                      label: `${sk.name} (${energyAmountText(1 + extra)}E/${combatInt((1 + extra) * 10)}D)`,
                    });
              } else
                for (const tar of enemyTargets(g, t))
                  a.push({
                    type: "skill",
                    skill: sk.id,
                    actor: who,
                    target: tar,
                    label: sk.name,
                  });
            }
          }
          return a;
        }
        if (!status(u, "paralyze"))
          a.push({ type: "gather", label: "에너지 모으기" });
        if (frozen) return a;
        if (canSwitch(g, t, u))
          for (let i = 0; i < team.bench.length; i++) {
            let b = team.bench[i];
            if (b && !b.dead)
              a.push({
                type:"switch", bench:i, switchCost:switchCost(u),

              });

                    Math.max(0, 6 - Math.floor(allEnergy(target))),
                  );

              }

                  });

                type: "skill",
                skill: sk.id,

      function receiveOnlineEmoteAck(msg){
        if(msg?.protocol!==BATTLE_EMOTE_PROTOCOL)return false;
        const incomingMatch=String(msg?.matchId||""),localMatch=battleEmoteMatchId();if(incomingMatch&&localMatch&&incomingMatch!==localMatch)return false;
        const id=String(msg?.emoteId||""),entry=battleEmotePending.get(id);if(!entry)return true;
        cancelBattleEmoteRetry(entry.timerId);battleEmotePending.delete(id);return true;
      }

      function ensurePendingSummonIdentity(g,q,u){
        if(!q)return q;
        const battleId=g?.battleId||g?.matchId||"battle";
        const serial=Number(q.summonSerial??u?.summonSerial??0);
        q.actionId ||= `${battleId}:summon:${Number(g?.turnSerial||0)}:${q.summonUid}:${serial}`;
        q.transactionId ||= `${q.actionId}:tx`;
        q.submitState ||= "AWAITING_INPUT";
        return q;
      }
      function normalizePendingSummonTurns(g){
        g.pendingSummonTurns ||= [];
        while(g.pendingSummonTurns.length){
          const q=g.pendingSummonTurns[0], u=findUnit(g,q.summonUid), owner=findUnit(g,q.ownerUid);
          if(!u||u.dead||!u.isSummon||!owner||owner.dead||teamOf(g,u)!==q.team){g.pendingSummonTurns.shift();continue;}
          ensurePendingSummonIdentity(g,q,u);
          return {q,u,owner};
        }
        return null;
      }
      function summonSkillCost(g,t,u,skillRef){
        const sk=skillOf(u,skillRef);
        if(skillDefHasRuntimeKind(sk,"summonRageClaw")){
          const n=Math.max(0,(g.teams[t].deathSerial||0)-(u.deathBaseline||0));
          return '*'.repeat(Math.max(0,3-n));
        }
        return sk?.cost||'';
      }
      function summonTargetRefs(g,t,ally){
        const team=ally?t:other(t);
        return allBattleUnits(g,team,true).map(u=>({u,ref:targetRef(g,team,u)}));
      }
      function summonActionOptions(g,t,u){
        const out=[{type:'summonGather',label:'에너지 모으기'}];
        if(status(u,'freeze')||status(u,'paralyze'))return out;
        const skills={
          heal:firstSkillByRuntimeKind(u,"summonHeal"),
          peck:firstSkillByRuntimeKind(u,"summonEnergyPeck"),
          constrict:firstSkillByRuntimeKind(u,"summonConstrict"),
          rage:firstSkillByRuntimeKind(u,"summonRageClaw"),
        };
        const behavior=cardCapability(u,'summonBehavior','');
        if(behavior==='support'){
          const heal=skills.heal,healCost=summonSkillCost(g,t,u,heal);
          if(heal&&!u.skillSeals?.[heal.id]&&canPayAction(g,t,u,healCost))
            for(const {u:target} of summonTargetRefs(g,t,true))out.push({type:'summonSkill',skill:heal.id,targetUid:target.uid,label:`${heal.name} → ${target.name}`});
          const peck=skills.peck,peckCost=summonSkillCost(g,t,u,peck);
          if(peck&&!u.skillSeals?.[peck.id]&&canPayAction(g,t,u,peckCost))
            for(const {u:target} of summonTargetRefs(g,t,false))out.push({type:'summonSkill',skill:peck.id,targetUid:target.uid,label:`${peck.name} → ${target.name}`});
        }else if(behavior==='constrict'){
          const sk=skills.constrict,cost=summonSkillCost(g,t,u,sk);
          if(sk&&!u.skillSeals?.[sk.id]&&canPayAction(g,t,u,cost))
            for(const {u:target} of summonTargetRefs(g,t,false))out.push({type:'summonSkill',skill:sk.id,targetUid:target.uid,label:`${sk.name} → ${target.name}`});

      function submitManualSummonAction(a){
        const cur=normalizePendingSummonTurns(game); if(!cur||!a)return;
        const {q,u,owner}=cur;
        if(a.actionId&&a.actionId!==q.actionId)return;

        const canonicalAction={...a,actionId:q.actionId,transactionId:q.transactionId,sourceInstanceId:u.uid};
        if(game.mode==="online"&&p2p?.role==="guest"&&q.team===onlineSide&&!p2p.applyingRemoteAction){
          if(onlinePendingCommand||q.submitState!=="AWAITING_INPUT")return;
          q.submitState="SUBMITTED";
          q.submittedAction={type:canonicalAction.type||null,skill:canonicalAction.skill||null,targetUid:canonicalAction.targetUid||null,paymentPlan:canonicalAction.paymentPlan?{...canonicalAction.paymentPlan}:null};
          beginOnlinePendingCommand({
            type:"actionRequest",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:"PVP",commandKind:"summon",
            matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),actionId:q.actionId,transactionId:q.transactionId,
            expectedTurnSerial:game.turnSerial,expectedTurn:game.turn,team:q.team,summonUid:u.uid,ownerUid:owner.uid,
            action:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(canonicalAction))
          });
          return;
        }
        q.submitState="COMMITTING";
        if(applyManualSummonAction(game,q.team,u.uid,canonicalAction))afterAction();
        else {q.submitState="AWAITING_INPUT";renderActions("manual-summon-rejected");}
      }

      function renderManualSummonActions(viewModel){

        }
      }

      }

      function rawReqFromCost(cost) {
        const tokens = costTokens(cost), req = { R:0, W:0, B:0, any:0, wb:0 };
        for (const x of tokens) {
          if (x === "*") req.any++;
          else if (x === "WorB") req.wb++;
          else req[x]++;
        }
        return { req, tokens, text: tokens.join("") };
      }

      function timeMarkTimeline(g, type, payload = {}) {
        if (!g) return null;
        g.clockTimeline ||= [];
        const mark = {
          type,
          at: performance.now(),
          turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          activeEntityId: g.activeEntityId || unitAt(g, ...(g.order?.[g.turn] || []))?.uid || null,
          ...payload,
        };
        g.clockTimeline.push(mark);
        if (g.clockTimeline.length > 160) g.clockTimeline.splice(0, g.clockTimeline.length - 160);
        return mark;
      }
      function timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g) {
        const pending = g?.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        if (!pending) return false;
        delete g.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;
        const actor = findUnit(g, pending.actorEntityId) || unitAt(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex);
        g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance = true;
        try {
          if (actor) advanceDualOrEnd(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, actor, pending.usedSkill, pending.switched);
          else endTurn(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, pending.usedSkill);
        } finally {
          delete g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance;
        }
        if (window.StateFactory?.updateActive) window.StateFactory.updateActive(g);
        timeMarkTimeline(g, "turnAdvanced", { fromTurnSerial:pending.turnSerial, toTurnSerial:Number(g.turnSerial || 0) });
        timeMarkTimeline(g, "nextActorAssigned", { nextActorEntityId:g.activeEntityId || null, nextTeamId:g.activeTeamId || null });
        g._timeAwaitingNextAiAction = !!(
          !g.winner &&
          ((g.mode === "human" && g.activeTeamId === "A") || g.mode === "aivai" || (g.bossRaid && g.activeTeamId === "A"))
        );
        return true;
      }

      function timeNormDeg(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return ((n % 360) + 360) % 360; }
      function timeIsAligned(u) {
        if (!u || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;
        const d = timeNormDeg((u.timeHourDeg || 0) - (u.timeMinuteDeg || 0));
        return Math.abs(d) < .001 || Math.abs(d - 180) < .001;
      }

      class TimeClockOwnershipDomain {
        constructor({ id, team, owners, suppressedUnits = [], shared = false, label = "운명의 시계" }) {
          this.id = String(id || "clock-domain");
          this.team = team || null;
          this.shared = !!shared;
          this.label = label;
          this.owners = (owners || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.ownerUids = new Set(this.owners.map(u => String(u.uid)));
          this.suppressedUnits = (suppressedUnits || []).filter(Boolean);
          this.suppressedUids = new Set(this.suppressedUnits.map(u => String(u.uid)));
        }
        owns(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.ownerUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        suppresses(unitOrUid) {
          const uid = typeof unitOrUid === "object" ? unitOrUid?.uid : unitOrUid;
          return uid != null && this.suppressedUids.has(String(uid));
        }
        representative() { return this.owners[0] || null; }
      }

            const stamp = `${g.turnSerial}:${domain.id}:${[...domain.ownerUids].join(",")}`;
            if (g._timeSuppressionLogStamp !== stamp) {
              g._timeSuppressionLogStamp = stamp;
              const stoppedUnits = timeFieldUnits(g).filter(x => domain.suppresses(x));
              const stopped = stoppedUnits.map(x => x.name);
              log(g,`${domain.label} 정렬: ${stopped.join(", ") || "대상 없음"}의 특성 발동 정지.`,"turn");
              for (const x of timeFieldTimeoras(g).filter(x => domain.suppresses(x)))
                log(g,`${x.name}: 상대 운명의 시계로 시계 반응·시간 가속·타임프로텍트·특성 정지 기능 정지.`,"turn");
              domain.representative();
            }
          }
          timeSyncContinuousTraitStates(g);
        }
      }

      function legalActionsCore(g, t, s) {

      function maskedStateFor(g, viewer) {
        let c = clone(g),
          enemy = other(viewer),
          known = g.revealed[enemy] || [],
          knownIds = new Set(
            [
              ...g.teams[enemy].field,
              ...g.teams[enemy].graveyard,
              ...g.teams[enemy].bench.filter((u) => u && known.includes(u.uid)),
            ]
              .filter(Boolean)
              .map((u) => u.card),
          ),
          pool = poolForLeague(g.league).filter((id) => !knownIds.has(id));
        c.knowledge = { viewer, unknownCandidates: pool };
        c.teams[enemy].bench = c.teams[enemy].bench.map((u, i) =>
          u && known.includes(u.uid)
            ? u
            : u
              ? {
                  ...makeUnit("mole", `hidden-${enemy}-${i}`),
                  card: "unknown",
                  name: "미공개 카드",
                  hp: 190,
                  maxHp: 190,
                  hidden: true,
                }
              : null,
        );
        return c;
      }
      function simExecute(g, t, s, a) {
        let c = clone(g);
        c.rng = new RNG(1);
        c.logs = [];
        execute(c, t, s, a, { sim: true });
        return c;
      }
      function search(g, root, depth, alpha, beta, tt, deadline) {
        if (depth <= 0 || g.winner || performance.now() > deadline)
          return evaluate(g, root);
        let [t, s] = g.order[g.turn],
          acts = legalActions(g, t, s);
        if (!acts.length) {
          let c = clone(g);
          endTurn(c, t, s, false);
          return search(c, root, depth - 1, alpha, beta, tt, deadline);
        }
        acts.sort(
          (a, b) => actionHeuristic(g, t, s, b) - actionHeuristic(g, t, s, a),
        );
        acts = acts.slice(0, 8);
        let max = t === root,
          best = max ? -Infinity : Infinity,
          key = stateKey(g, depth);
        if (tt.has(key)) return tt.get(key);
        for (const a of acts) {
          let v;
          if (a.type === "gather") {
            v = 0;
            for (const color of ["R", "W", "B"])
              v +=
                search(
                  simExecute(g, t, s, { ...a, color }),
                  root,
                  depth - 1,
                  alpha,
                  beta,
                  tt,
                  deadline,
                ) / 3;
          } else
            v = search(
              simExecute(g, t, s, a),
              root,
              depth - 1,
              alpha,
              beta,
              tt,
              deadline,
            );
          if (max) {
            best = Math.max(best, v);
            alpha = Math.max(alpha, v);
          } else {
            best = Math.min(best, v);
            beta = Math.min(beta, v);
          }
          if (beta <= alpha || performance.now() > deadline) break;
        }
        tt.set(key, best);
        return best;
      }
      function chooseAIBase(g, t, s, quick = false) {
        let acts = legalActions(g, t, s),
          u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        if (!acts.length) return null;
        if (
          cardHasCapability(u, "ignoreStatChanges") &&
          !status(u, "poison") &&
          acts.some((a) => a.type === "skill" && actionSkillHasTag(a, "free"))
        )
          acts = acts.filter((a) => a.type !== "gather");
        if (
          u &&
          totalEnergy(u) >= (card(u).maxEnergy || 4) &&
          gatherPotential(u) < 0 &&
          acts.some((a) => a.type === "skill" || a.type === "scheduled")
        )
          acts = acts.filter((a) => a.type !== "gather");
        let scored = acts
          .map((a) => ({ a, h: actionHeuristic(g, t, s, a) }))
          .sort((x, y) => y.h - x.h);
        if (quick) return scored[0].a;
        let maxDepth = g.difficulty === "hard" ? 4 : 2,
          deadline = performance.now() + (g.difficulty === "hard" ? 120 : 60),
          tt = new Map(),
          best = scored[0];
        for (let d = 1; d <= maxDepth; d++) {
          if (performance.now() > deadline) break;
          let roundBest = null,
            complete = true;
          for (const x of scored.slice(0, 8)) {
            if (performance.now() > deadline) {
              complete = false;
              break;
            }
            let v =
              search(
                simExecute(g, t, s, x.a),
                t,
                d - 1,
                -Infinity,
                Infinity,
                tt,
                deadline,
              ) +
              x.h * 0.08;
            if (!roundBest || v > roundBest.v) roundBest = { ...x, v };
          }
          if (complete && roundBest) best = roundBest;
        }
        best.a.reason = aiReason(g, t, s, best.a);
        return best.a;
      }
      function applyLifeRainAiSplit(g, t, action) {
        if (!actionSkillHasEffect(action, "lifeRain")) return action;
        const allies = g.teams[t].field.filter(x => x && !x.dead);
        const values = allies.map(x => Math.max(1, (x.maxHp - x.hp) * 2 + x.lastAttackDamage + (x.hp < 80 ? 80 : 0)));
        const sum = values.reduce((n, x) => n + x, 0);
        action.split = allies.length < 2 ? 30 : Math.round((30 * values[0]) / sum);
        return action;
      }
      function imminentBloom(g, t) {
        return (
          g.teams[other(t)].field.find(
            (x) =>
              x &&
              !x.dead &&
              !!firstSkillByEffect(x, "bloomingLife") &&
              !x.scheduledAction &&
              !status(x, "freeze") &&
              !status(x, "skillSeal") &&
              canPay(x, "W*"),
          ) || null
        );
      }
      function leavesPaidResponse(u, a) {
        if (a.type !== "skill") return true;
        let copy = clone(u),
          sk = card(copy).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          cost = a.costOverride ?? sk?.cost ?? "",
          actor = a.actor || null;
        if (!canPayFrom(copy, cost, actor)) return false;
        spendFrom(copy, cost, a.prefer, actor);
        return card(copy).skills.some(
          (x) => !skillDefHasEffect(x, "bloomingLife") && canPayFrom(copy, x.cost, actor),
        );
      }
      function expectedActionDamage(g, t, u, a, target) {
        if (a.type !== "skill" || !target) return 0;
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          e = sk?.effects.find((x) => x.type === "damage");
        if (e) return damageRange(g, u, target, e, sk).mean * (e.hits || 1);
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "prawnCharge"))
          return hasNullifyTrait(target) ? 160 : 120;
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "firestorm"))
--- legalActions #5 offset=529336 ---
               card: "unknown",
                  name: "미공개 카드",
                  hp: 190,
                  maxHp: 190,
                  hidden: true,
                }
              : null,
        );
        return c;
      }
      function simExecute(g, t, s, a) {
        let c = clone(g);
        c.rng = new RNG(1);
        c.logs = [];
        execute(c, t, s, a, { sim: true });
        return c;
      }
      function search(g, root, depth, alpha, beta, tt, deadline) {
        if (depth <= 0 || g.winner || performance.now() > deadline)
          return evaluate(g, root);
        let [t, s] = g.order[g.turn],
          acts = legalActions(g, t, s);
        if (!acts.length) {
          let c = clone(g);
          endTurn(c, t, s, false);
          return search(c, root, depth - 1, alpha, beta, tt, deadline);
        }
        acts.sort(
          (a, b) => actionHeuristic(g, t, s, b) - actionHeuristic(g, t, s, a),
        );
        acts = acts.slice(0, 8);
        let max = t === root,
          best = max ? -Infinity : Infinity,
          key = stateKey(g, depth);
        if (tt.has(key)) return tt.get(key);
        for (const a of acts) {
          let v;
          if (a.type === "gather") {
            v = 0;
            for (const color of ["R", "W", "B"])
              v +=
                search(
                  simExecute(g, t, s, { ...a, color }),
                  root,
                  depth - 1,
                  alpha,
                  beta,
                  tt,
                  deadline,
                ) / 3;
          } else
            v = search(
              simExecute(g, t, s, a),
              root,
              depth - 1,
              alpha,
              beta,
              tt,
              deadline,
            );
          if (max) {
            best = Math.max(best, v);
            alpha = Math.max(alpha, v);
          } else {
            best = Math.min(best, v);
            beta = Math.min(beta, v);
          }
          if (beta <= alpha || performance.now() > deadline) break;
        }
        tt.set(key, best);
        return best;
      }

      function expectedActionDamage(g, t, u, a, target) {
        if (a.type !== "skill" || !target) return 0;
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill),
          e = sk?.effects.find((x) => x.type === "damage");
        if (e) return damageRange(g, u, target, e, sk).mean * (e.hits || 1);
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "prawnCharge"))
          return hasNullifyTrait(target) ? 160 : 120;
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "firestorm")) return 80;
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "holyFlame"))
          return Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(target));
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "shatterLance"))
          return 70 + status(target, "freeze") * 30;
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "rampage")) return (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10;
        return 0;
      }
      /* Core AI owner: historically split across 12 nested wrappers. Keep one ordered layer so
         character additions feed shared capabilities/skills instead of deepening the call chain. */
            function actionHeuristicCore(g, t, s, a) {
        let sc = actionHeuristicBase(g, t, s, a),
          u = unitAt(g, t, s),
          tar = a?.target ? unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot) : null,
          sk = a?.type === "skill" ? skillOf(u, a.skill) : null;
        if (!u) return sc;

        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "holyFlame") && a.healTargetUid) {
          const h = findUnit(g, a.healTargetUid),
            recovery = 100 - Math.max(0, 100 - 20 * allEnergy(tar));
          sc += Math.min(recovery, Math.max(0, h.maxHp - h.hp)) * 1.2;
        }
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "rampage") && canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) {
          const safeExtra = Math.max(0, totalEnergy(u, "beast") - 1),
            damage = (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10;
          if ((a.extra || 0) > safeExtra && tar.hp > damage) sc -= 180;
        }
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "bloomingLife")) {
          const danger = g.teams[other(t)].field.reduce((n, x) => n + (x && !x.dead
            ? Math.max(...

ffect(a, "hurricaneDive") && tar) {
          const d = 30 + 70 * (a.extraW || 0), delay = a.extraW || 0,
            risk = delay * (u.hp < u.maxHp * 0.4 ? 55 : 25) + g.teams[other(t)].bench.filter(x => x && !x.dead).length * delay * 10;
          sc += d - risk + (d >= tar.hp + tar.shield ? 110 : 0);
        }
        if (actionSkillHasRuntimeKind(a, "selfHealStatusHit") && tar) sc += Math.min(30, u.maxHp - u.hp) * 1.3 + (isStatusImmune(tar, "slow") ? 0 : 28);
        if (a.type === "gather" && cardHasCapability(u, "eclipse") && eclipseBlocks(u)) sc += 28;
        if (a.type === "switch" && u.scheduledAction?.kind === "zigzag" && g.fieldEffect?.ownerUid === u.uid) sc -= 180;

        const aw = imminentBloom(g, t);
        if (aw) {
          if (a.type === "gather") sc += 65;
          if (actionPaysEnergy(u, a) && !leavesPaidResponse(u, a))
            sc -= tar?.uid === aw.uid && expectedActionDamage(g, t, u, a, aw) >= aw.hp + aw.shield ? 0 : 120;
          if (tar?.uid === aw.uid) sc += 75;
        }

        if (a.type === "switch") {
          sc -= switchCost(u) * 32;
          if (cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") && activeIruma(g, other(t))) sc += 210;
        }

        if (!!firstSkillByEffect(u, "mysticBurst") && a.type === "gather" && totalEnergy(u) < 3) sc += 95 - totalEnergy(u) * 15;
        if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "mysticBurst") && tar) {
          const x = a.extraColors || { R: 0, W: 0, B: 0 },
            mean = (100 + 30 * x.R) * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
            healValue = Math.min(50 * x.W, Math.max(0, u.maxHp - u.hp)) * 1.25;
          let statusValue = x.B * (isStatusImmune(tar, "poison") && isStatusImmune(tar, "slow") ? 10 : 34);
          if (activeIruma(g, other(t))) statusValue -= x.B * 28;
          sc += mean + healValue + statusValue + (mean >= tar.hp + tar.shield ? 160 : 0) - 12 * (x.R + x.W + x.B);
        }
        sc += scoreSkillAiProfile(g, u, tar, a, sk);
        return sc;
      }

            function chooseAICore(g, t, s, quick = false) {
        let a = chooseAIBase(g, t, s, quick), u = unitAt(g, t, s);
        if (!u || u.dead) return a;

        if (cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor")) {
          const acts = legalActions(g, t, s), who = g.dualActorController?.actor || "beast";
          if (who === "beast") {
            const roar = acts.find(x => actionSkillHasEffect(x, "iceRoar"));
            if (roar && canPayFrom(u, "WR*", "rider")) a = roar;
            else if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "rampage") && (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10 <= 20 &&
              !acts.some(x => actionSkillHasEffect(x, "rampage") && unitAt(g, x.target.team, x.target.slot)?.hp <= (1 + (x.extra || 0)) * 10)) {
              const gather = acts.find(x => x.type === "gather");
              if (gather && costGap(u, "WB", "beast") > 0) a = gather;
            }
          } else {
            const lances = acts.filter(x => actionSkillHasEffect(x, "shatterLance") && status(unitAt(g, x.target.team, x.target.slot), "freeze"));
            if (lances.length) {
              lances.sort((x, y) => status(unitAt(g, y.target.team, y.target.slot), "freeze") - status(unitAt(g, x.target.team, x.target.slot), "freeze"));
              a = lances[0];
            }
          }
        }
        if (pendingBloom(g, t) && !actionPaysEnergy(u, a)) {
          const paid = legalActions(g, t, s).filter(x => actionPaysEnergy(u, x))
            .sort((x, y) => actionHeuristic(g, t, s, y) - actionHeuristic(g, t, s, x));
          if (paid.length && actionHeuristic(g, t, s, paid[0]) > actionHeuristic(g, t, s, a)) a = paid[0];
        }
        applyLifeRainAiSplit(g, t, a);

        const bloom = pendingBloom(g, t);
        if (bloom) {
          const acts = legalActions(g, t, s),
            focus = acts.filter(x => x.target && unitAt(g, x.target.team, x.target.slot)?.uid === bloom.uid)
              .sort((x, y) => actionHeuristic(g, t, s, y) - actionHeuristic(g, t, s, x)),
            lethal = focus.find(x => expectedActionDamage(g, t, u, x, bloom) >= bloom.hp + bloom.shield),
            paid = acts.filter(x => actionPaysEnergy(u, x)).sort((x, y) => actionHeuristic(g, t, s, y) - actionHeuristic(g, t, s, x)),
            paidFocus = focus.filter(x => actionPaysEnergy(u, x));
          if (lethal) a = lethal;
          else if (paidFocus.length) a = paidFocus[0];
          else if (paid.length) a = paid[0];
          else if (focus[0] && actionHeuristic(g, t, s, focus[0]) > actionHeuristic(g, t, s, a)) a = focus[0];
        }

        if (cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") && (g.dualActorController?.actor || "beast") === "beast") {
          const acts = legalActions(g, t, s), roar = acts.find(x => actionSkillHasEffect(x, "iceRoar"));
          if (roar) a = roar;
          else if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "rampage")) {
            const tar = unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot), damage = (1 + (a.extra || 0)) * 10,
              gather = acts.find(x => x.type === "gather");
            if (tar && tar.hp > damage && damage < 50 && gather && costGap(u, "WB", "beast") > 0) a = gather;
          }
        }

        if (cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor") && activeIruma(g, other(t)) && (g.dualActorController?.actor || "beast") === "beast") {
          const swaps = legalActions(g, t, s).filter(x => x.type === "switch")
            .sort((x, y) => actionHeuristic(g, t, s, y) - actionHeuristic(g, t, s, x));
          if (swaps.length) a = swaps[0];
        }

        const energyTradeSkillIds = new Set(card(u).skills.filter(sk => sk.aiProfile?.kind === "mutualEnergyTrade").map(sk => sk.id));
        if (!!firstSkillByEffect(u, "mysticBurst") || energyTradeSkillIds.size) {
          const acts = legalActions(g, t, s), gather = acts.find(x => x.type === "gather"),
            canHuveSkill = acts.some(x => actionSkillHasEffect(x, "mysticBurst") || energyTradeSkillIds.has(x.skill) || actionSkillHasTag(x, "fieldClear"));
          if (!canHuveSkill && gather) {
            const safeKill = acts.filter(x => energyTradeSkillIds.has(x.skill)).some(x => {
              const tar = unitAt(g, x.target.team, x.target.slot), sk = skillOf(u, x.skill), profile = sk?.aiProfile || {},
                n = Number(x.interactionAmount || 0), mean = Number(profile.baseDamage || 0) * (g.variance ? 0.83 : 1),
                per = Number(profile.perEnergyDamage || 0), selfPer = Number(profile.selfDamagePerEnergy || per);
              return mean + per * n >= tar.hp + tar.shield && selfPer * n < u.hp;
            });
            if (!safeKill && (totalEnergy(u) < 5 || gatherPotential(u) > 0)) a = gather;
          }
        }
        return a;
      }

        if (a.type === "scheduled" || a.type === "sequence")

        if (a.type === "dualPass") return `다음 복합 행동의 에너지를 보존했다.`;
        if (a.type === "switch")
          return cardHasCapability(u, "dualActor

              a = lances[0];

          return `${u.name}의 실제 기술 완성에 도움이 되는 에너지를 준비했다.`;

        if (sk?.target === "enemySlot")
          return `후열 출전을 압박할 슬롯에 ${sk.name}을 설치했다.`;

      }

      function clearLocalAutoTurnTimer(reason = "clear") {
        if (localAutoTurnTimerId) window.TimerManager.clear(localAutoTurnTimerId);
        localAutoTurnTimerId = 0;
        if (localAutoTurnOwner) localAutoTurnOwner.clearedReason = String(reason);
        localAutoTurnOwner = null;
        return true;
      }
      function scheduleLocalAutoTurnTimer(kind, delay, callback) {
        clearLocalAutoTurnTimer("replace");
        const owner = {
          gameRef: game, token: game?.token, serial: game?.turnSerial,
          battleId: game?.battleId || null, kind: String(kind || "automatic-turn"),
          scheduledAt: Date.now(),
        };
        localAutoTurnOwner = owner;
        localAutoTurnTimerId = window.TimerManager.setTimeout(() => {
          if (localAutoTurnOwner !== owner) return;
          localAutoTurnTimerId = 0;
          localAutoTurnOwner = null;
          callback(owner);
        }, Math.max(0, Number(delay) || 0));
        return owner;
      }

      const LocalInputEligibility = (() => {
        let providers = Object.freeze({
          locks: () => [],
          clock: () => ({ locked: false }),
          online: () => ({}),
          bossControls: () => true,
        });
        const bindProviders = next => {
          providers = Object.freeze({ ...providers, ...(next || {}) });
          return true;
        };
        const evaluate = (g, context = null) => {
          const reasons = [];
          const surfaceKind = context?.surfaceKind || (g?.bossRaid ? "boss-raid" : "base");
          const pair = context?.actorTeam != null
            ? [context.actorTeam, context.actorSlot ?? null]
            : (g?.order?.[g?.turn] || []);
          const currentActorCanonicalTeam = pair[0] ?? null;
          const currentActorSlot = pair[1] == null ? null : pair[1];
          const actor = context?.actor || (currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null
            ? unitAt(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot)
            : null);
          const currentActorUid = context?.actorUid || actor?.entityId || actor?.uid || null;
          const canonicalLocalTeam = context?.canonicalLocalTeam || (g ? localTeamFor(g) : null);
          const displayLocalSide = canonicalLocalTeam ? displaySideFor(g, canonicalLocalTeam) : null;
          const standardOnline = context?.standardOnline ?? (g?.mode === "online" && !g?.bossRaid?.online);
          const online = providers.online?.(g) || {};
          const runtime = online.runtime || {};
          const locks = [...(providers.locks?.() || [])];
          const clock = providers.clock?.() || {};
          let actions = [];
          try {
            if (Array.isArray(context?.actions)) actions = [...context.actions];
            else if (g && currentActorCanonicalTeam != null && currentActorSlot != null) actions = legalActions(g, currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot) || [];
          }
          catch (error) { reasons.push("LEGAL_ACTION_EVALUATION_FAILED"); }
          const replacementRequired = (!actor || actor.dead) && actions.some(action => action.type === "deploy");
          const mandatoryAutomaticAction = actions.some(action => action.type === "scheduled" || action.type === "speedDelay");
          const manualActions = actions.filter(action => action.type !== "scheduled" && action.type !== "speedDelay");

          if (!g) reasons.push("NO_GAME");
          else {
            if (g.ended || g.winner) reasons.push("BATTLE_ENDED");
            if (!pair.length) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_MISSING");
            if ((!actor || actor.dead) && !replacementRequired) reasons.push("CURRENT_ACTOR_NOT_ALIVE");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeTeamId && g.activeTeamId !== currentActorCanonicalTeam) reasons.push("ACTIVE_TEAM_MISMATCH");
            if (!context?.skipActiveIdentityChecks && g.activeEntityId && currentActorUid && g.activeEntityId !== currentActorUid) reasons.push("ACTIVE_ENTITY_MISMATCH");
            if (g.mode === "spectator") reasons.push("SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY");
            if (g.mode === "aivai" || g.mode === "ai-ai") reasons.push("AI_CONTROLLED_MODE");
            if (currentActorCanonicalTeam !== canonicalLocalTeam) reasons.push("REMOTE_ACTOR_TURN");
            if (standardOnline) {
              const expectedTeam = online.role === "host" ? "P" : online.role === "guest" ? "A" : null;
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (expectedTeam && canonicalLocalTeam !== expectedTeam) reasons.push("ROLE_CANONICAL_TEAM_MISMATCH");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && !runtime.committed) reasons.push("SESSION_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE") reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "UNKNOWN"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online) {
              if (!online.connected) reasons.push("TRANSPORT_NOT_CONNECTED");
              if (!online.battleStarted) reasons.push("BATTLE_START_NOT_COMMITTED");
              if (runtime.generation > 0 && (!runtime.committed || runtime.state !== "IN_BATTLE")) reasons.push(`SESSION_${runtime.state || "NOT_COMMITTED"}`);
              if (online.reconnecting) reasons.push("RECONNECTING");
            }
            if (g?.bossRaid?.online && !providers.bossControls?.(g)) reasons.push("BOSS_SLOT_NOT_LOCAL");
            if (locks.length) reasons.push("ACTIVE_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (clock.locked) reasons.push("CLOCK_INPUT_LOCK");
            if (g.pendingTransaction || g.pendingAction || g.pendingPayment || context?.pendingSubmitted) reasons.push("PENDING_AUTHORITATIVE_ACTION");
            if (online.pendingActionId || context?.pendingActionId) reasons.push("PENDING_ONLINE_ACTION");
            if (online.busy) reasons.push("APPLICATION_BUSY");
            if (!actions.length) reasons.push("NO_LEGAL_ACTIONS");
            if (mandatoryAutomaticAction) reasons.push("MANDATORY_AUTOMATIC_ACTION");
          }
          const uniqueReasons = [...new Set(reasons)];
          const ownsCurrentActor = !!g && currentActorCanonicalTeam === canonicalLocalTeam && !["SPECTATOR_READ_ONLY", "AI_CONTROLLED_MODE"].some(reason => uniqueReasons.includes(reason));
          const canAct = ownsCurrentActor && uniqueReasons.length === 0 && manualActions.length > 0;
          return Object.freeze({
            canAct, reasons: uniqueReasons, ownsCurrentActor,
            canonicalLocalTeam, displayLocalSide,
            currentActorCanonicalTeam, currentActorSlot, currentActorUid,
            currentActorCharacterId: actor?.card || null,
            activeEntityId: g?.activeEntityId || null,
            actorAlive: !!actor && !actor.dead, replacementRequired,
            mandatoryAutomaticAction,
            legalActionCount: actions.length,
            manualLegalActionCount: manualActions.length,
            gatherLegal: actions.some(action => action.type === "gather"),
            actions,
            activeLocks: locks.map(lock => ({ ...lock })),
            pendingActionId: online.pendingActionId || g?.pendingAction?.actionId || null,
            authoritativeRevision: Number(online.authoritativeRevision || 0),
            localAppliedRevision: Number(g?.networkRevision || 0),
            sessionState: runtime.state || null,
            reconnectState: online.reconnecting ? "RECONNECTING" : "CONNECTED",
            surfaceKind,
          });
        };
        return Object.freeze({ evaluate, canAct: g => evaluate(g).canAct, bindProviders });
      })();

          // PvP automatic commands are game rules, not AI turns. The host alone executes them
          // and sends the resulting authoritative state to both clients.
          if (!forced || p2p?.role !== "host") return;
          if (onlineAutoCommandOwner) return;
          const automaticOwner = {
            game,
            token,
            serial,
            actionId: forcedAction?.actionId || `${game.battleId || game.matchId}:automatic:${serial}:${noLegalActions ? "no-legal-actions" : forcedAction?.type || "forced"}`,
          };

          onlineAutoCommandActive = true;

            }

          forced ? `scheduled:${forcedAction?.type || "forced"}` : "ai-turn",
          true ? Math.max(Number($("#speed")?.value || 900), visualHoldMs) : 0,

              } else executed = a ? execute(game, t, s, a) : false;
              if (a && executed && reason) log(game, `AI 판단: ${reason}`, "aiwhy");
            } catch (error) {

              if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.error("[local auto-turn]", error);
            }
            if (!executed) {

                if (fallback) executed = execute(game, t, s, fallback);
              } catch (error) {
                window.__megaRecordRecoveredError?.(error, "scheduleAI.local-fallback");
              }
              if (!executed) {
                log(game, `${u?.name || "빈 슬롯"}: AI 행동 처리 실패, 턴을 안전하게 종료.`, "turn");
                endTurn(game, t, s, false);
              }
            }
            busy = false;
            render();
            try {
              if (!processPendingTraitChoice()) scheduleAI();
            } catch (error) {
              window.__megaRecordRecoveredError?.(error, "scheduleAI.local-continuation");
              busy = false;
              renderActions();
              scheduleAI();
            }
          },
        );

      function startBattle(seedOverride, onlineConfig = null) {
        const isOnline = onlineConfig?.mode === "online";
        const onlineScope = isOnline ? String(
          onlineConfig?.matchId || p2p?.matchId || seedOverride || onlineConfig?.coinResult?.id || p2p?.roomCode || "online"
        ) : null;

          return game;

          ? { ...ONLINE_COMMON_RULES, ...(onlineConfig?.rules || p2p.rules || {}), variance: false, difficulty: "normal" }
          : null;

          fallbackComposition = battleLeague === "comprehensive"
            ? LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(isOnline ? (p2p?.localComposition || sharedRules.composition || comprehensiveComposition) : comprehensiveComposition)
            : comprehensiveComposition;

            battleLeague,
            onlineConfig?.leagueCompositions || onlineConfig?.compositions || sharedRules?.compositions || {
              P: fallbackComposition,
              A: isOnline ? (p2p?.remoteComposition || null) : fallbackComposition,
            },
            { P:pDeck, A:aDeck },
            battlePlusStar,
          ),
          battleComposition = battleCompositions.P;
        if (
          !LeagueFormatOwner.completeValid(pDeck, battleLeague, battleCompositions.P, battlePlusStar?.P || null, "P") ||
