      function severeDeckConflict(ids) {
        return (
          ids.includes("flameflower") &&
          (ids.includes("kingprawn") || ids.includes("iceknight"))
        );
      }
      function randomBattleDeck(r, league = "mega", composition = comprehensiveComposition) {
        league = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeLeague(league);
        composition = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(composition);
        let pool = [...poolForLeague(league, composition)], d = [];
        while (d.length < 4) {
          const valid = pool.filter(id => LeagueFormatOwner.partialValid([...d, id], league, composition)),
            compatible = league === "mega" ? valid.filter(id => !severeDeckConflict([...d, id])) : valid,
            choices = compatible.length ? compatible : valid;
          if (!choices.length) throw new Error(`random deck composition exhausted: ${league}/${composition}/${d.join(",")}`);
          const id = r.pick(choices);
          d.push(id);
          pool = pool.filter(x => x !== id);
        }
        return league === "mega" ? arrangeAIDeck(d) : d;
      }
      function quickBattle(seed, variance, stats, league = "mega", composition = comprehensiveComposition) {
        composition = LeagueFormatOwner.normalizeComposition(composition);
        let r = new RNG(seed),
          pd = randomBattleDeck(r, league, composition),
          ad = randomBattleDeck(r, league, composition),
          g = newGame(pd, ad, {
            seed,
            variance,
            difficulty: "normal",
            mode: "aivai",
            league,
            leagueComposition: composition,
          }),
          limit = 5000;
        while (!g.winner && limit--) {
          let [t, s] = g.order[g.turn],
            view = maskedStateFor(g, t),
            a = chooseAI(view, t, s, true);
          let acted = a ? execute(g, t, s, a, { sim: false }) : false;
          if (!acted) {
            const fallbacks = legalActions(g, t, s);
            for (const fallback of fallbacks) {
              if (execute(g, t, s, fallback, { sim: false })) { acted = true; break; }
            }
          }
          if (!acted) endTurn(g, t, s, false);
        }
        if (!g.winner) {
          if (stats.__meta) stats.__meta.timeouts++;
          g.winner = evaluate(g, "P") >= 0 ? "P" : "A";
          log(g, `5000행동 상한 판정승: ${g.winner}.`, "turn");
        }
        if (stats.__meta) {
          stats.__meta.totalActions =
            (stats.__meta.totalActions || 0) + g.stats.turns;
          stats.__meta.maxActions = Math.max(
            stats.__meta.maxActions || 0,
            g.stats.turns,
          );
          if (g.stats.turns >= 400)
            stats.__meta.longBattles = (stats.__meta.longBattles || 0) + 1;
        }
        for (const [t, deck] of [
          ["P", pd],
          ["A", ad],
        ])
          for (const id of deck) {
            let st = stats[id],
              won = g.winner === t;
            st.picks++;
            st.wins += won;
            let units = [
              ...g.teams[t].field,
              ...g.teams[t].bench,
              ...g.teams[t].graveyard,
            ].filter((x) => x && x.card === id);
            for (const u of units) {
              st.turns += u.turns;
              st.dealt += u.damageDealt;
              st.taken += u.damageTaken;
              st.skills += u.skillUses;
              st.status += u.statusScore;
              st.reflect += u.reflectDealt;
              st.absorb += u.shieldAbsorbed;
              st.disrupt += u.energyDisrupt;
              st.switches += u.switches;
              st.roars = (st.roars || 0) + (u.roarUses || 0);
              st.memories = (st.memories || 0) + (u.memoryUses || 0);
              st.bloomTargets = (st.bloomTargets || 0) + (u.bloomTargets || 0);
              st.bloomEvaded = (st.bloomEvaded || 0) + (u.bloomEvaded || 0);
            }
          }
        return g.winner;
      }
      async function runSimulation() {

            function chooseAISpecialist(g, t, s, quick = false) {
        const oldTimeGame = timeTraitGame;
        timeTraitGame = g;
        try {
          let a = chooseAICore(g, t, s, quick), u = unitAt(g, t, s);
          if (!a || !u) return a;

          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "suctionPad") && a.target) {
            const target = unitAt(g, a.target.team, a.target.slot);
            if (target) a.rDiscardSpecs = bestDiscardSpecs(target, 1);
          }
          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "eternaHeal")) {
            const allies = g.teams[t].field.filter(x => x && !x.dead),
              firstNeed = Math.max(0, (allies[0]?.maxHp || 0) - (allies[0]?.hp || 0)),
              secondNeed = Math.max(0, (allies[1]?.maxHp || 0) - (allies[1]?.hp || 0));
            a.healSplitPct = firstNeed + secondNeed ? Math.round(100 * firstNeed / (firstNeed + secondNeed)) : 50;
            const rTarget = allies.slice().sort((x, y) => energyValue(y) - energyValue(x))[0] || u;
            a.rTargetUid = rTarget.uid;
            a.rDiscardSpecs = bestDiscardSpecs(rTarget, 1);
          }
          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "toxiousUnseal")) a.unsealDiscardSpecs = bestDiscardSpecs(u, 3);
          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "abyssVortex")) {
            const plan = findPaymentPlan(g, t, u, "B");
            a.paymentPlan = plan;
            a.vortexSwaps = bestVortexSwaps(g, t, u, plan);
          }
          if (isNonAttackSkill(card(u).skills.find(x => x.id === a.skill))) {
            const dm = chooseDeepMindTarget(g, t);
            if (dm) a.deepMindTargetUid = dm.uid;
          }

          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "lifeRain")) {
            const allies = g.teams[t].field.filter(x => x && !x.dead), pool = lifeRainHealPool(g, u, t),
              need0 = Math.max(0, (allies[0]?.maxHp || 0) - (allies[0]?.hp || 0)),
              need1 = Math.max(0, (allies[1]?.maxHp || 0) - (allies[1]?.hp || 0));
            a.split = allies.length < 2 ? pool : Math.round(pool * (need0 / (need0 + need1 || 2)));
          }
          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "abyssVortex")) {
            const pairs = legalActions(g, t, s).filter(x => actionSkillHasEffect(x, "abyssVortex"));
            a = pairs.sort((x, y) => {
              const ax = x.vortexTargets.map(id => findUnit(g, id)), ay = y.vortexTargets.map(id => findUnit(g, id));
              return ay.reduce((n, z) => n + allEnergy(z), 0) - ax.reduce((n, z) => n + allEnergy(z), 0);
            })[0] || a;
            a.paymentPlan = findPaymentPlan(g, t, u, "B");
            const [A, B] = (a.vortexTargets || []).map(id => findUnit(g, id));
            a.vortexSwaps = [];
            if (A && B && !circuitBlocksExternal(A, u) && !circuitBlocksExternal(B, u)) {
              const sa = vortexEnergySlots(A), sb = vortexEnergySlots(B);
              outer: for (const ea of sa) for (const eb of sb) if (ea.c !== eb.c) {
                a.vortexSwaps = [[{uid:A.uid, actor:ea.actor, index:ea.index}, {uid:B.uid, actor:eb.actor, index:eb.index}]];
                break outer;
              }
            }
          }

          if (actionSkillHasEffect(a, "foxStrike") && !!firstSkillByEffect(u, "foxStrike")) {
            if (a.strikeMode === "double" && u.strikeSingleColor && colorAmount(u, u.strikeSingleColor) >= 2 - 1e-6) a.prefer = [u.strikeSingleColor, u.strikeSingleColor];
            else if (a.strikeMode === "single" && u.strikeSingleColor) a.prefer = [u.strikeSingleColor];
          }
          return a;
        } finally {
          timeTraitGame = oldTimeGame;
        }
      }

      function chooseAI(g, t, s, quick = false) {
        const boss = window.__redTimeoraBossRaid;
        const fallback = () => chooseAISpecialist(g, t, s, quick);
        if (g?.bossRaid && typeof boss?.chooseAIController === "function")
          return boss.chooseAIController(g, t, s, quick, fallback);
        if (g?.bossRaid) return boss?.searchChooseR32?.(g,t,s,quick) || boss?.basicChoose?.(g,t,s,quick) || fallback();
        return fallback();
      }

        function onlineConnectionReady() {
          const open=!!p2p?.conn?.open;
          if(open&&p2p&&!p2p.connected)p2p.connected=true;

        function sendOnlinePacketSafe(packet) {
          try {
            if (!onlineConnectionReady()) return false;
            return p2p.conn.send(packet) !== false;
          } catch (error) {
            if (window.DEBUG_BATTLE_EVENTS) console.warn("[online-resume] send failed", packet?.type, error);
            return false;
          }

        function retryReliableOnlineState(force = false) {
          if (!onlineConnectionReady() || p2p?.role !== "host" || game?.mode !== "online") return;
          const packet = p2p.__reliableStatePacket;
          if (!packet) return;
          if (Number(p2p.__reliableStateAck || 0) >= Number(packet.revision || 0)) return;
          if (!force && Date.now() - Number(p2p.__reliableStateSentAt || 0) < 720) return;
          // Reliable retries are immutable: state/turn must stay paired with packet.revision.
          p2p.__reliableStateSentAt = Date.now();
          sendOnlinePacketSafe(packet);
        }

bossHooks.actionHeuristic = function(bossHeuristicBefore,g,t,s,a){
      let score=bossHeuristicBefore(g,t,s,a),u=unitAt(g,t,s);
      if(!g?.bossRaid||!u?.bossRed)return score;
      score+=bossPhaseActionScore(g,t,s,a);
      if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "bossTimeStrike"))score+=Number(a.bossTimeStrikeDamage||40)*.9;
      if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "hyperClock")){
        const target=a.target?unitAt(g,a.target.team,a.target.slot):null,raw=bossClockPhase(g).aligned?160:100;
        score+=raw+(target&&target.hp+target.shield<=raw?430:0);
      }
      if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "timeRecall")&&a.target){
        const target=unitAt(g,a.target.team,a.target.slot),v=timeRecallValue(target);
        score+=(a.target.team===t?v*1.35:-v*1.2)+Math.max(0,v)*.5;
      }
      return score;
    };

function bossChooseBasic(g,t,s,deep=false){
      const u=unitAt(g,t,s);
      if(!g?.bossRaid||!u?.bossRed)return null;
      if(u.scheduledAction)return {type:"scheduled"};
      const actions=legalActions(g,t,s),phase=bossClockPhase(g);
      const hypers=actions.filter(a=>actionSkillHasEffect(a, "hyperClock")),recalls=actions.filter(a=>actionSkillHasEffect(a, "timeRecall"));
      const bestHyper=bossBestAction(g,t,s,hypers,a=>{const z=a.target?unitAt(g,a.target.team,a.target.slot):null;return z&&z.hp+z.shield<=(phase.aligned?160:100)?500:0;});
      if(phase.aligned&&bestHyper)return bestHyper;
      const recallRank=recalls.map(a=>({a,value:timeRecallValue(a.target?unitAt(g,a.target.team,a.target.slot):null),plan:bossActionPhasePlan(g,t,s,a)})).sort((x,y)=>(y.value+(y.plan.after.aligned?170:0))-(x.value+(x.plan.after.aligned?170:0)));
      if(recallRank[0]?.value>=145)return recallRank[0].a;
      const ranked=actions.map(a=>({a,score:actionHeuristic(g,t,s,a),plan:bossActionPhasePlan(g,t,s,a)})).sort((x,y)=>y.score-x.score);
      const best=ranked[0];
      if(best)return best.a;
      return null;
    }

    function bossHandlePacket(msg){
      if(!msg||typeof msg!=="object")return false;
      if(msg.type==="bossLobbyState"&&msg.protocol===BOSS_LOBBY_PROTOCOL){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||p2p?.battleStarted)return false;
        if(msg.matchId&&p2p.matchId&&msg.matchId!==p2p.matchId)return false;
        p2p.bossRemoteDeck=bossSanitizeDeck(msg.deck,2);
        p2p.bossRemotePlusStar=resolvePlusStarChoice(msg.plusStar,p2p.bossRemoteDeck,"A");
        p2p.bossRemoteReady=!!msg.ready&&bossDeckValid(p2p.bossRemoteDeck,2);
        showBossOnlineLobby();bossMaybeStartOnline();return true;
      }
      if(msg.type==="bossRewardOffer"&&p2p.role==="guest"){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||!game?.bossRaid?.online||msg.mode!=="BOSS_COOP"||String(msg.matchId||"")!==String(p2p.matchId||game.matchId||""))return false;
        if(game.pendingTraitChoices?.[0]?.serial!==msg.reward?.serial){game.pendingTraitChoices ||= [];game.pendingTraitChoices.unshift(msg.reward);}
        processPendingTraitChoice();return true;
      }
      if(msg.type==="bossRewardPick"&&p2p.role==="host"){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||!game?.bossRaid?.online||msg.mode!=="BOSS_COOP"||String(msg.matchId||"")!==String(p2p.matchId||game.matchId||""))return false;
        const q=game?.pendingTraitChoices?.[0];
        if(q?.kind==="bossReward"&&Number(q.serial)===Number(msg.serial))bossFinishReward(q,Number(msg.index),msg.pickerRole||"guest");
        else bossSendState(true);
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    }

  function featureVector(g){
    const A=living(g?.teams?.A),P=living(g?.teams?.P),ph=clockInfo(g);
    let recall=0,scheduled=0,low=0;
    for(const u of A){
      if(!u.usedOnce?.[skillIdByEffect(u, "timeRecall")]){try{recall=Math.max(recall,Number(timeRecallValue(u)||0));}catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "bossTimeoraRLPlannerR32:L62"); }}
      if(u.scheduledAction)scheduled++;
      if((Number(u.hp)||0)<=80)low++;
    }
    return {
      bias:1,bossHp:teamEhp(g?.teams?.A)/100,playerHp:teamEhp(g?.teams?.P)/100,
      bossAlive:A.length,playerAlive:P.length,bossEnergy:A.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0),playerEnergy:P.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0),
      bossStatus:A.reduce((n,u)=>n+statusLoad(u),0),playerStatus:P.reduce((n,u)=>n+statusLoad(u),0),
      aligned:ph.aligned?1:0,phaseDistance:ph.distance,playerOneCostTrap:ph.one?1:0,playerTwoCostTrap:ph.two?1:0,
      recallValue:recall/100,scheduled,bossLowHp:low
    };
  }
  function dot(f,w=W){ let v=0; for(const [k,x] of Object.entries(f))v+=(Number(w[k])||0)*(Number(x)||0); return v; }
  function baseValue(g){
    if(g?.winner)return g.winner==="A"?1e6:g.winner==="P"?-1e6:0;
    return dot(featureVector(g));
  }

  function actionKey(a){return [a?.type,a?.skill,a?.costOverride,a?.target?.team,a?.target?.slot,a?.target?.uid,a?.extraW,JSON.stringify(a?.paymentPlan||null),JSON.stringify(a?.split||null)].join("|");}
  function paidUnits(g,t,u,a){
    if(a?.type!=="skill")return 0;
    try{const n=Number(timePaidAmountFromAction(g,t,u,a)||0);if(n>0)return Math.ceil(n-1e-9);}catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "bossTimeoraRLPlannerR32:L101"); }

  }
  function expanded(g,t,s){
    const u=unitAt(g,t,s),raw=legalActions(g,t,s)||[],out=[];
    for(const a of raw){
      if(a?.type==="gather"&&bossUnit(g,u,t)){out.push({...a,timeAccelerationChoice:"keep"},{...a,timeAccelerationChoice:"reroll"});}
      else out.push(a);
    }
    const seen=new Set();return out.filter(a=>{const k=actionKey(a);if(seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true;});
  }
  function transitionDanger(before,after){
    const bA=living(before.teams.A),aA=living(after.teams.A);
    const hpLoss=Math.max(0,teamEhp(before.teams.A)-teamEhp(after.teams.A));
    const deaths=Math.max(0,bA.length-aA.length);
    const statusGain=Math.max(0,aA.reduce((n,u)=>n+statusLoad(u),0)-bA.reduce((n,u)=>n+statusLoad(u),0));
    const energyLoss=Math.max(0,bA.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0)-aA.reduce((n,u)=>n+energy(u),0));
    const playerGain=Math.max(0,teamEhp(after.teams.P)-teamEhp(before.teams.P));
    return hpLoss*1.05+deaths*520+statusGain*46+energyLoss*18+playerGain*.42;
  }
  function nextPlayerThreats(g){
    const turn=g?.order?.[g.turn]; if(!turn||turn[0]!=="P")return {skills:[],none:true};
    const [t,s]=turn,u=unitAt(g,t,s);if(!u||u.dead)return {skills:[],none:true};
    const skills=[];
    for(const a of expanded(g,t,s)){
      if(a?.type!=="skill")continue;
      const after=sim(g,t,s,a);if(!after)continue;
      let likelihood=0;try{likelihood=Number(actionHeuristic(g,t,s,a)||0);}catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "bossTimeoraRLPlannerR32:L128"); }
      const danger=transitionDanger(g,after),units=paidUnits(g,t,u,a),aligned=clockInfo(after).aligned;
      skills.push({action:a,danger,likelihood,units,aligned,score:danger+Math.max(-80,Math.min(220,likelihood))*.18});
    }
    skills.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
    return {skills,none:skills.length===0};
  }
  function trapScore(afterBoss){
    const q=nextPlayerThreats(afterBoss);
    if(q.none){
      const ph=clockInfo(afterBoss);
      return {score:(ph.aligned?260:0)-ph.distance*28,none:true,target:null,covered:false};
    }
    const top=q.skills.slice(0,Math.min(6,q.skills.length));
    let total=0,best=top[0],covered=false;
    for(let i=0;i<top.length;i++){
      const x=top[i],weight=1/(1+i*.55);
      if(x.aligned){total+=x.score*2.35*weight;if(x===best)covered=true;}
      else total-=x.score*(x===best?1.25:.38)*weight;
    }
    return {score:total,none:false,target:best,covered};
  }
  function immediate(g,t,s,a){
    const child=sim(g,t,s,a);if(!child)return -Infinity;
    let score=baseValue(child)-baseValue(g);
    if(t==="A")score+=trapScore(child).score;
    if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "bossTimeStrike"))score+=Number(a.bossTimeStrikeDamage||0)*.28;
    if(actionSkillHasEffect(a, "hyperClock"))score+=clockInfo(g).aligned?4.8:1.5;
    return score;
  }
  function ordered(g,t,s,limit){
    const arr=expanded(g,t,s).map(a=>({a,v:immediate(g,t,s,a)})).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.v));
    arr.sort((x,y)=>t==="A"?y.v-x.v:x.v-y.v);
    return arr.slice(0,limit);
  }
  function stateKey(g,d){
    const c=g?.bossRaid?.sharedClock||{};
    const us=["P","A"].map(t=>[...(g.teams[t]?.field||[]),...(g.teams[t]?.bench||[])].map(u=>u?`${u.uid}:${u.hp}:${u.shield}:${u.dead?1:0}:${energy(u)}:${Object.values(u.statuses||{}).join(',')}:${u.lastSkill||''}`:'-').join(';')).join('|');
    return `${d}|${g.turn}|${g.turnSerial||0}|${c.hourDeg||0},${c.minuteDeg||0}|${g.rng?.s||0}|${us}`;
  }
  function search(g,d,alpha,beta,deadline,tt){
    if(d<=0||g?.winner||performance.now()>=deadline)return baseValue(g);
    const turn=g?.order?.[g.turn];if(!turn)return baseValue(g);
    const [t,s]=turn,key=stateKey(g,d);if(tt.has(key))return tt.get(key);
    const max=t==="A",list=ordered(g,t,s,max?8:7);
    if(!list.length){const c=copyGame(g);try{endTurn(c,t,s,false);}catch (error) { window.__megaRecordRecoveredError(error, "bossTimeoraRLPlannerR32:L173"); }const v=search(c,d-1,alpha,beta,deadline,tt);tt.set(key,v);return v;}
    let best=max?-Infinity:Infinity;
    for(const x of list){if(performance.now()>=deadline)break;const c=sim(g,t,s,x.a);if(!c)continue;let v=search(c,d-1,alpha,beta,deadline,tt);if(max)v+=trapScore(c).score*.08;
      if(max){best=Math.max(best,v);alpha=Math.max(alpha,best);}else{best=Math.min(best,v);beta=Math.min(beta,best);}if(beta<=alpha)break;}
    if(!Number.isFinite(best))best=baseValue(g);tt.set(key,best);return best;
  }

      const PlusStarBannerPresentationOwner = (() => {
        const pending = new Set(), bindings = new WeakMap();
        const scopeKey = context => `${context.surface || "composition"}|${context.scope || "local"}|${context.team || "P"}`;
        const choiceKey = (context, choice) => `${scopeKey(context)}|${choice?.slotIndex ?? -1}|${choice?.cardId || "none"}`;
        const queueActivation = (context, choice) => { if(choice)pending.add(choiceKey(context,choice)); };
        const burst = (banner, identity) => {
          if(!banner || banner.querySelector(':scope > .plusstar-particle-layer'))return false;
          const layer=document.createElement('span');layer.className='plusstar-particle-layer';layer.dataset.plusstarBurst=identity;
          banner.appendChild(layer);
          const animations=[];
          for(let i=0;i<16;i++){
            const particle=document.createElement('span');particle.className='plusstar-gold-particle';
            const angle=(Math.PI*2*i/16)+(i%3)*.09, radius=34+(i%5)*8, dx=Math.cos(angle)*radius,dy=Math.sin(angle)*radius;
            particle.style.setProperty('--particle-size',`${4+(i%4)*2}px`);layer.appendChild(particle);
            if(typeof particle.animate==='function')animations.push(particle.animate([
              {opacity:0,transform:'translate(-50%,-50%) scale(.25)'},
              {opacity:1,transform:`translate(calc(-50% + ${dx*.28}px),calc(-50% + ${dy*.28}px)) scale(1.15)`,offset:.26},
              {opacity:.78,transform:`translate(calc(-50% + ${dx*.74}px),calc(-50% + ${dy*.74}px)) scale(.72)`,offset:.7},
              {opacity:0,transform:`translate(calc(-50% + ${dx}px),calc(-50% + ${dy}px)) scale(.12)`},
            ],{duration:620+(i%4)*70,delay:(i%5)*22,easing:'cubic-bezier(.12,.66,.25,1)',fill:'forwards'}).finished.catch(()=>{}));
          }
          Promise.allSettled(animations).then(()=>layer.remove());
          return true;
        };
        const sync = (root, context, deck, choice) => {
          if(!root)return false;
          const resolved=resolvePlusStarChoice(choice,deck,context.team||'P');
          root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="toggle-plusstar"]').forEach(button=>{
            const cardId=button.dataset.plusstarCard,banner=button.closest('.card,.boss-deck-card');
            const explicit=Number(banner?.dataset?.infoSlotIndex),slotIndex=Number.isInteger(explicit)&&explicit>=0?explicit:deck.indexOf(cardId);
            const effectiveCard=EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.resolve(card(cardId),null,{deck,plusStarChoice:choice,team:context.team||"P",slotIndex,surface:context.surface||"composition",contextIdentity:scopeKey(context)});
            const active=effectiveCard.plusStar;
            button.classList.toggle('active',active);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active));
            EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.syncElement(banner,effectiveCard);
            if(!active)banner?.querySelector(':scope > .plusstar-particle-layer')?.remove();
            if(active){const key=choiceKey(context,resolved);if(pending.delete(key))burst(banner,key);}
          });
          if (typeof refreshCardTooltip === "function") refreshCardTooltip();
          return true;
        };
        const bind = (root, handler) => {
          if(!root)return false;
          bindings.set(root,handler);
          if(root.dataset.plusstarDelegated==='true')return true;
          root.dataset.plusstarDelegated='true';
          root.addEventListener('click',event=>{
            const button=event.target.closest?.('[data-action="toggle-plusstar"]');
            if(!button||!root.contains(button))return;
            event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
            const current=bindings.get(root);if(typeof current==='function')current(button,event);
          });
          return true;
        };
        return Object.freeze({queueActivation,sync,bind});
      })();
      const PlusStarInteractionOwner = Object.freeze({
        apply({surface,scope,team='P',deck,currentChoice,cardId,capacity=4,validate=validFormDeck,commit,feedback}) {
          const beforeDeck=[...(deck||[])],beforeChoice=resolvePlusStarChoice(currentChoice,beforeDeck,team),candidate=CARDS.find(c=>c.id===cardId);
          const reject=message=>{if(typeof feedback==='function')feedback(message);return Object.freeze({ok:false,message,deck:beforeDeck,choice:beforeChoice});};
          if(!candidate||!PLUS_STAR_ELIGIBLE.has(cardId))return reject('PlusStar를 사용할 수 없는 캐릭터입니다.');
          let nextDeck=[...beforeDeck],slotIndex=nextDeck.indexOf(cardId),nextChoice=null,activated=false;
          if(slotIndex>=0&&beforeChoice?.slotIndex===slotIndex&&beforeChoice?.cardId===cardId)nextChoice=null;
          else{
            if(slotIndex<0){
              if(nextDeck.length>=capacity)return reject('덱이 가득 차 PlusStar 캐릭터를 선택할 수 없습니다.');
              const proposal=[...nextDeck,cardId];
              if(!validate(proposal,null))return reject('기존 덱 규칙 때문에 이 캐릭터를 선택할 수 없습니다.');
              nextDeck=proposal;slotIndex=nextDeck.length-1;
            }
            nextChoice=resolvePlusStarChoice({slotIndex,cardId},nextDeck,team);
            if(!nextChoice)return reject('PlusStar exact slot identity를 확정하지 못했습니다.');
            activated=true;
          }
          if(!validate(nextDeck,nextChoice))return reject('덱 검증에 실패했습니다.');
          if(typeof commit!=='function')return reject('편성 commit owner가 없습니다.');
          commit(nextDeck,nextChoice);
          const context={surface,scope,team};
          if(activated)PlusStarBannerPresentationOwner.queueActivation(context,nextChoice);
          return Object.freeze({ok:true,activated,deactivated:!nextChoice,deck:[...nextDeck],choice:nextChoice,context});
        }
      });

      function makeUnit(id, uid) {
        let __result;
        __makeUnitCore: {
        const c = card(id), baseDefense = c.baseDefense ?? 0,
          openingGuardSkill = firstSkillByRuntimeKind(c, "openingGuard"),
          strikeSkill = firstSkillByEffect(c, "foxStrike"),
          copySkill = firstSkillByTag(c, "copy"),
          recallSkill = firstSkillByEffect(c, "timeRecall");
        const u = {
          uid, entityId:uid, card:id, name:c.name, deckSlotId:null, plusStar:false,
          hp:c.maxHp, maxHp:c.maxHp, shield:c.shield || 0,
          baseDefense, defenseMod:0, defense:baseDefense, attackMod:0,
          energy:[], beastEnergy:[], riderEnergy:[], dualInitialized:false,
          everDeployed:false, speedDelayTurns:0, eclipseEligible:true,
          statuses:{}, skillSeals:{}, hideHits:0, hpProtectionBarriers:[], barrierTurnEpoch:0,
          queuedHeals:[], sequence:null, scheduledAction:null, damageTransfer:null,
          fireWingUntil:null, fullInvulnerableUntilOwnTurn:false,
          queuedSkillCostReduction:0, turnSkillCostReduction:0,
          yggThresholdArmed:!!c.thresholdEnergy,
          firstGuardUsesRemaining:openingGuardSkill ? 1 : 0,
          usedOnce:{}, dead:false, lastAction:null, lastTarget:null, lastSkill:null,
          lastAttackDamage:0, turns:0, damageDealt:0, damageTaken:0,
          healingDealt:0, healingTaken:0, skillUses:0, statusScore:0,
          reflectDealt:0, shieldAbsorbed:0, energyDisrupt:0, switches:0,
        };
        if (strikeSkill) { u.strikeSingleColor=null; u.strikeUses=0; }
        if (copySkill) {
          u.memoryStrikeSingleColor=null; u.memoryStrikeUses=0; u.memoryUsed=false; u.memoryUses=0;
        }
        if (c.timeControl) {
          u.timeHourDeg=0; u.timeMinuteDeg=90; u.timeRecallHistory=[]; if (recallSkill) u.usedOnce[recallSkill.id]=false;
        }
        { __result = u; break __makeUnitCore; }
      
        }
        const unit=__result;
        if (unit) {
          unit.entityId ||= unit.uid||uid;
          unit.uid ||= unit.entityId;
          if (typeof EnergyEngine!=="undefined") EnergyEngine.ensureStore(unit);
        }
        return unit;
      }

      function renderSetup(reset = true) {
        if (reset) DeckDraftApplication.reset();
        normalizeSetupFormsForLeague();
        const ids = LeagueFormatOwner.setupPool(leagueSelection, comprehensiveComposition, selected, plusStarSelection, "P");
        $("#deckCards").innerHTML = ids
          .map((id) => {
            const c = CARDS.find((x) => x.id === id);
            if (!c) return "";
            const slotIndex = selected.indexOf(c.id),
              effectiveCard = EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.resolve(c, null, {deck:selected,plusStarChoice:plusStarSelection,team:"P",slotIndex,surface:"local-composition"}),
              plusActive = effectiveCard.plusStar,
              legalForms = c.formGroup ? LeagueFormatOwner.eligibleForms(c.formGroup, selected, leagueSelection, comprehensiveComposition, plusStarSelection, "P") : [],
              nextForm = c.formGroup ? nextLeagueFormId(c.formGroup, c.id, selected, leagueSelection, comprehensiveComposition, plusStarSelection, "P") : null,
              chosen = selected.includes(c.id),
              slotBlocked = !chosen && !LeagueFormatOwner.partialValid([...selected, c.id], leagueSelection, comprehensiveComposition, plusStarSelection, "P"),
              toggle = c.formGroup && legalForms.length > 1 && nextForm
                ? `<button class="form-toggle" data-form-toggle="${c.formGroup}">${c.name} → ${CARDS.find(x => x.id === nextForm)?.name || c.name}</button>`
                : "",
              plus = PLUS_STAR_ELIGIBLE.has(c.id)
                ? `<button class="plusstar-toggle${plusActive ? " active" : ""}" data-action="toggle-plusstar" data-plusstar-card="${c.id}" data-plus-star="${c.id}" aria-pressed="${plusActive}">🎆</button>`
                : "",
              formInfo = c.formGroup && legalForms.length
                ? `<div class="form-eligibility"><b>폼</b>${legalForms.map(fid => { const fc=CARDS.find(x=>x.id===fid); return `<span class="form-chip">${fc?.name || fid} ${fc?.grade || ""}</span>`; }).join("")}</div>`
                : "";
            return `<div class="card compact-card selectable ${effectiveCard.visualModifiers.glowClass} ${slotBlocked ? "league-slot-blocked" : ""}" data-info-card="setup" data-info-surface="local-composition" data-info-team="P" data-info-slot-index="${slotIndex}" data-card-id="${c.id}" data-id="${c.id}" data-effective-character-id="${effectiveCard.effectiveCharacterId}" data-effective-plus-star="${effectiveCard.plusStar}" data-effective-deck-slot="${effectiveCard.exactDeckSlot || ""}" aria-disabled="${slotBlocked}">${pictogramHTML("small", effectiveCard.effectiveCharacterId)}<h3 class="card-title-line"><span>${effectiveCard.displayName}</span><span class="tiny">${effectiveCard.rarity}</span>${toggle}${plus}</h3><div class="stats"><span>HP ${c.maxHp}</span><span>실드 ${c.shield || 0}</span>${(c.baseDefense || 0) !== 0 ? `<span>방어 ${c.baseDefense || 0}</span>` : ""}<span>최대E ${c.maxEnergy}</span>${c.speed ? `<span>Speed ${c.speed > 0 ? "+" : ""}${c.speed}</span>` : ""}</div>${formInfo}<div class="tiny compact-role"><b>${c.role}</b></div></div>`;
          })
          .join("");
        $$('[data-form-toggle]').forEach(button => {
          button.onclick = event => {
            event.stopPropagation();
            const group = button.dataset.formToggle,
              current = button.closest('[data-card-id]')?.dataset.cardId || setupFormState(group),
              next = nextLeagueFormId(group, current, selected);
            if (!next || next === current || !DeckDraftApplication.changeForm(group, next)) return;
            renderSetup(false);
          };
        });
        $$("#deckCards .card").forEach(cardEl => {
          cardEl.onclick = event => {
            if (event?.target?.closest?.(".form-toggle,.plusstar-toggle")) return;
            if (cardEl.classList.contains("league-slot-blocked")) return;
            if (!DeckDraftApplication.toggleCard(cardEl.dataset.id, 4)) return;
            updateDeckSetup();
          };
        });
        updateDeckSetup();
        mountInvaderPortal();
        updateDeckSetup();
      }
      function deckConflictPenalty(ids) {
        if (!ids.includes("flameflower")) return 0;
        let penalty = 0;
        if (ids.includes("kingprawn")) penalty -= 80;
        if (ids.includes("iceknight")) penalty -= 70;
        if (ids.includes("dragonfish")) penalty -= 35;
        return penalty;
      }
      function deckScore(ids) {
        let cs = ids.map((id) => CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)),
          roles = new Set(cs.map((c) => c.role.split(" ")[0])).size,
          expensive = cs
            .flatMap((c) => c.skills)
            .filter(
              (s) =>
                parseCost(s.cost).any +
                  parseCost(s.cost).R +
                  parseCost(s.cost).W +
                  parseCost(s.cost).B >=
                3,
            ).length,
          support = cs.filter((c) =>
            c.skills.some((s) =>
              s.tags.some((t) => ["heal", "shield", "support"].includes(t)),
            ),
          ).length,
          tanks = cs.filter((c) => c.role.includes("탱커")).length,
          cheap = cs.filter((c) =>
            c.skills.some(
              (s) =>
                s.tags.includes("damage") &&
                costGap(makeUnit(c.id, "score"), s.cost) <= 1,
            ),
          ).length;
        return (
          roles * 10 +
          Math.min(1, support) * 12 +
          Math.min(1, tanks) * 12 +
          Math.min(2, cheap) * 5 +
          (expensive ? support * 6 : 0) +
          deckConflictPenalty(ids)
        );
      }
      function arrangeAIDeck(ids) {
        let d = [...ids],
          memoryIndex = d.findIndex((id) =>
            CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.skills.some(
              (s) => skillDefHasTag(s, "copy"),
            ),
          );
        if (memoryIndex < 0) return d;
        let memoryId = d[memoryIndex],
          dragonIndex = d.findIndex(
            (id, i) =>
              i !== memoryIndex &&
              CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.tags.includes("dragon"),
          );
        if (dragonIndex < 0) return d;
        let dragonId = d[dragonIndex],
          rest = d.filter((_, i) => i !== memoryIndex && i !== dragonIndex);
        return [memoryId, ...rest.slice(0, 1), dragonId, ...rest.slice(1)];
      }
      function validFormDeck(ids) {
        let groups = ids
          .map((id) => CARDS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.formGroup)
          .filter(Boolean);
        return new Set(groups).size === groups.length &&
          new Set(ids).size === ids.length &&
          (ids || []).filter(isInvaderCardId).length <= 1;
      }

          });

bossHooks.scheduleAI = function(bossScheduleBefore){
      if(!game?.bossRaid)return bossScheduleBefore();
      if(game.winner||busy||game.pendingTraitChoices?.length)return;
      prepareCurrentTurn(game);
      const [t,s]=game.order[game.turn],u=unitAt(game,t,s),actions=legalActions(game,t,s),noLegalActions=actions.length===0;
      if(game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="guest"){busy=false;renderActions();return;}
      if(t==="P"&&!noLegalActions){renderActions();return;}
      const token=game.token,serial=game.turnSerial;
      busy=true;renderActions();
      let onlineTimerId=0;
      const run=()=>{
        if(game?.bossRaid?.online&&bossOnlineAiTimer!==onlineTimerId)return;
        bossOnlineAiTimer=0;
        if(!game||game.token!==token||game.turnSerial!==serial){busy=false;return;}
        if(noLegalActions){
          log(game,`${u?.name||"빈 슬롯"}: 가능한 행동이 없어 턴을 자동으로 넘김.`,"turn");
          endTurn(game,t,s,false);busy=false;afterAction();return;
        }
        const view=maskedStateFor(game,t),liveVisualEvents=visualEvents,liveVisualHoldMs=visualHoldMs;
        let a=null,reason="";
        try{visualEvents=[];visualHoldMs=0;a=u?.scheduledAction?{type:"scheduled"}:chooseAI(view,t,s,false);if(a&&!u?.scheduledAction)reason=aiReason(view,t,s,a)||"";}
        finally{visualEvents=liveVisualEvents;visualHoldMs=liveVisualHoldMs;}
        let ok=a?execute(game,t,s,a):false;
        if(a&&ok&&reason)log(game,`보스 AI 판단: ${reason}`,"aiwhy");
        if(!ok){const fallback=legalActions(game,t,s)[0];if(fallback)ok=execute(game,t,s,fallback);}
        if(!ok)endTurn(game,t,s,false);
        busy=false;afterAction();
      };
      const delay=Math.max(520,Number(document.getElementById("speed")?.value||900));
      if(game.bossRaid.online){if(bossOnlineAiTimer&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(bossOnlineAiTimer))window.TimerManager.clear(bossOnlineAiTimer);onlineTimerId=window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setTimeout?.(run,delay,"boss-online-ai",{phase:"SESSION"})||window.TimerManager.setTimeout(run,delay);bossOnlineAiTimer=onlineTimerId;}
      else scheduleLocalAutoTurnTimer(noLegalActions?"boss-no-legal-actions":"boss-ai",delay,run);
    };

    function bossRevive(raw){const g=JSON.parse(raw),rng=new RNG(g.seed);rng.s=g.rng?.s||rng.s;g.rng=rng;g.token=++battleToken;g.mode="bossOnline";bossSyncClock(g);return g;}
    function bossSendState(force=false,visuals=[]){
      if(!game?.bossRaid)return false;
      const packetVisuals=cloneOnlineVisualEvents(Array.isArray(visuals)?visuals:[]);
      const canHostSpectators=!game.bossRaid.online||(p2p.role==="host"&&p2p.connected);
      let sent=false;
      if(game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="host"&&p2p.connected){
        const ack=p2p?.__pendingAuthoritativeAck||null;
        if(p2p)p2p.__pendingAuthoritativeAck=null;
        sent=sendAuthoritativeOnlineState(packetVisuals,ack,force);
      }
      const spectator=window.__megaSpectatorR18;
      if(spectator&&canHostSpectators){
        const spectatorVisuals=packetVisuals.length?packetVisuals:cloneOnlineVisualEvents(visualEvents);
        if(spectatorVisuals.length)spectator.queueVisuals?.(spectatorVisuals);
        window.TimerManager.setTimeout(()=>{spectator.broadcastAnnounce?.();spectator.broadcastSnapshot?.(!!force);},0);
      }
      return sent;
    }

        render();

      bossSendState(false,teammateVisuals);
      if(!processPendingTraitChoice())scheduleAI();
    };

    function bossVisibleNormalPool(){
      const out=[],seenGroups=new Set();
      for(const rawId of bossRaidPool()){
        if(typeof isInvaderCardId==="function"&&isInvaderCardId(rawId))continue;
        const raw=CARDS.find(x=>x.id===rawId);if(!raw)continue;
        const id=raw.formGroup?(setupFormState(raw.formGroup)||raw.id):raw.id;
        const cardDef=CARDS.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!cardDef)continue;
        if(cardDef.formGroup){if(seenGroups.has(cardDef.formGroup))continue;seenGroups.add(cardDef.formGroup);}
        if(!out.includes(id))out.push(id);
      }
      return out;
    }
    function bossDeckCardHTML(id,selected,plusActive,surface="boss-composition"){
      const c=CARDS.find(x=>x.id===id),next=c?.formGroup?otherFormId(c.id):null;

      const slotIndex=bossRaidSelection.indexOf(id),effectiveCard=EffectiveCardPresentationOwner.resolve(c,null,{deck:bossRaidSelection,plusStarChoice:bossPlusStarSelection,team:"P",slotIndex,surface});
      const plusToggle=PLUS_STAR_ELIGIBLE.has(id)?`<button type="button" class="plusstar-toggle ${effectiveCard.plusStar?"active":""}" data-action="toggle-plusstar" data-plusstar-card="${id}" data-boss-plus-star="${id}" aria-pressed="${effectiveCard.plusStar}">🎆</button>`:"";
      return `<div class="boss-deck-card ${selected?"selected":""} ${effectiveCard.visualModifiers.glowClass}" data-info-card="setup" data-info-surface="${surface}" data-info-team="P" data-info-slot-index="${slotIndex}" data-card-id="${id}" data-boss-card="${id}" data-effective-character-id="${effectiveCard.effectiveCharacterId}" data-effective-plus-star="${effectiveCard.plusStar}" data-effective-deck-slot="${effectiveCard.exactDeckSlot || ""}">${pictogramHTML("small",effectiveCard.effectiveCharacterId)}<h3 class="card-title-line"><span>${effectiveCard.displayName}</span><span class="tiny">${effectiveCard.rarity}</span></h3><div class="tiny muted">HP ${c.maxHp} · 실드 ${c.shield||0}</div>${formToggle}${plusToggle}</div>`;
    }

      const ActionRenderEngine = (() => {
        const routes = new Map(), decorators = new Map();
        let renderSerial = 0, lastRender = null;
        const ordered = map => [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        const registerRoute = (id,priority,when,render) => {
          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});
          return ()=>routes.delete(String(id));
        };
        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {
          if(!id||typeof decorate!=="function"||typeof when!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render decorator");
          decorators.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,decorate,when});
          return ()=>decorators.delete(String(id));
        };
        const context = viewModel => {
          const pair=game?.order?.[game.turn]||[];
          return {game,team:pair[0],slot:pair[1],unit:pair.length?unitAt(game,pair[0],pair[1]):null,box:document.getElementById("actionButtons"),viewModel};
        };
        const render = (viewModel = null, requestReason = "unspecified") => {
          const panelView = viewModel || ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          let ctx=context(panelView),result;
          const route=ordered(routes).find(entry=>entry.when(ctx));
          result=route?route.render(ctx):renderBaseActions(panelView);
          ctx=context(panelView);
          for(const entry of ordered(decorators))if(entry.when(ctx))entry.decorate(ctx,result);
          const projection=panelView.projection;
          const buttons=[...(ctx.box?.querySelectorAll("button")||[])];
          const actionButtons=buttons.filter(button=>!button.dataset.actionPanelMeta);
          const fingerprint=actionButtons.map(button=>`${button.disabled?"0":"1"}:${button.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()||""}`).join("|");
          lastRender=Object.freeze({
            renderSerial:++renderSerial,
            renderedAt:Date.now(),
            requestReason:String(requestReason||"unspecified"),
            routeId:route?.id||"base",
            battleId:panelView.battleId,
            sessionGeneration:Number(window.OnlineRuntime?.debug?.().generation||0),
            networkRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            authoritativeRevision:Number(projection.authoritativeRevision||0),
            actionPanelViewModelRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            renderedDomRevision:panelView.canonicalRevision,
            viewModelSignature:panelView.signature,
            turnSerial:panelView.turnSerial,
            actorUid:projection.currentActorUid||null,
            actorCanonicalTeam:projection.currentActorCanonicalTeam||null,
            localCanonicalTeam:projection.canonicalLocalTeam||null,
            canAct:projection.canAct,
            reasons:[...projection.reasons],
            legalActionCount:Number(projection.legalActionCount||0),
            manualLegalActionCount:Number(projection.manualLegalActionCount||0),
            buttonCount:buttons.length,
            actionButtonCount:actionButtons.length,
            enabledButtonCount:actionButtons.filter(button=>!button.disabled).length,
            buttonFingerprint:fingerprint,
            gatherRendered:actionButtons.some(button=>button.textContent?.includes("에너지 모으기")),
          });
          return result;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          registerRoute,registerDecorator,render,base:()=>ActionPanelConvergenceOwner.request("action-render-base-api",{force:true}),
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            renderSerial=0; lastRender=null;
            const box=document.getElementById("actionButtons"); if(box)box.innerHTML="";
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),renderSerial,lastRender:null});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({
            routes:ordered(routes).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),
            decorators:ordered(decorators).map(x=>({id:x.id,priority:x.priority})),
            renderSerial,
            lastRender:lastRender?{...lastRender,reasons:[...lastRender.reasons]}:null,
          }),
        });
      })();

      function combatInt(value) {

        let halves = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(value || 0) * 2)),
          whole = Math.floor(halves / 2),
          half = halves % 2;
        return `${whole || ""}${half ? "½" : ""}` || "0";
      }

      function syncGrandFieldBackground(g) {
        let arena = document.querySelector(".arena");
        if (!arena) return;

          let visualOwner = grandVisualOwner(g);
          arena.classList.add(`grand-${visualOwner}`);
          let good = visualOwner === "P" ? ".player-side" : ".ai-side",
            bad = visualOwner === "P" ? ".ai-side" : ".player-side";
          document.querySelector(good)?.classList.add("grand-benefit");
          document.querySelector(bad)?.classList.add("grand-penalty");
        }

      function renderLog() {
        let box = $("#log");
        const distanceFromBottom = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight;
        const followLatest = lastLog === 0 || distanceFromBottom <= 28;
        const previousTop = box.scrollTop;
        let mobileClose = box.querySelector(".mobile-log-close");
        if (!mobileClose) {
          mobileClose = document.createElement("button");
          mobileClose.type = "button";
          mobileClose.className = "mobile-log-close";
          mobileClose.textContent = "로그 닫기";
          mobileClose.setAttribute("aria-label", "전투 로그 닫기");
          mobileClose.onclick = (event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();
            setBattleLogOpen(false);
          };
          box.prepend(mobileClose);
        }
        for (; lastLog < game.logs.length; lastLog++) {
          const l = game.logs[lastLog] || {};
          const message = normalizeGameNumberText(l.msg);
          if (!message) continue;
          const d = document.createElement("div");
          d.className = l.cls || "";
          d.textContent = message;
          box.appendChild(d);
        }
        if (followLatest) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        else box.scrollTop = previousTop;
      }
      function actionButton(box, label, fn, cls = "") {
        let b = document.createElement("button");

        return b;

      function paymentCostForAction(u, a) {
        let sk = card(u).skills.find((x) => x.id === a.skill);
        return effectiveSkillCost(game, game?.order?.[game.turn]?.[0] || "P", u, sk, a.costOverride ?? (skillDefHasEffect(sk, "hurricaneDive") ? "W*" : sk?.cost || ""));
      }
      function residualEnergyEntries(u, actor, plan) {
        let left = energyStore(u, actor).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index }));
        for (const c of "RWB") {
          let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
          for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
            if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
            let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
            left[i].a -= take; need -= take;
          }
        }
        return left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001);
      }
      function residualDualEnergyEntries(u, baseActor, plan) {
        let out = [];
        for (const who of ["beast", "rider"]) {
          let left = energyStore(u, who).map((e, index) => ({ ...e, index, actor: who }));
          if (who === baseActor) {
            for (const c of "RWB") {
              let need = plan?.[c] || 0;
              for (let i = left.length - 1; i >= 0 && need > 0.001; i--) {
                if (left[i].c !== c) continue;
                let take = Math.min(left[i].a, need);
                left[i].a -= take;
                need -= take;
              }
            }
          }
          out.push(...left.filter((e) => e.a > 0.001));
        }
        return out;
      }

      const ActionRenderEngine = (() => {
        const routes = new Map(), decorators = new Map();
        let renderSerial = 0, lastRender = null;
        const ordered = map => [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        const registerRoute = (id,priority,when,render) => {
          if(!id||typeof when!=="function"||typeof render!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render route");
          routes.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number(priority)||0,when,render});
          return ()=>routes.delete(String(id));
        };
        const registerDecorator = (id,priority,decorate,when=()=>true) => {
          if(!id||typeof decorate!=="function"||typeof when!=="function")throw new TypeError("invalid action render decorator");
          decorators.set(String(id),{id:String(id),priority:Number
--- renderAction #4 offset=6103406 ---
signature:null,canonicalRevision:Number(game?.networkRevision||0),turnSerial:Number(game?.turnSerial||0),actorUid:null,dom:state,at:Date.now()});
          return lastRequest;
        };
        const request = (reason="action-panel-convergence", options={}) => {
          const view=ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game);
          const root=document.getElementById("actionButtons");if(root)delete root.dataset.actionPanelSuspended;
          const before=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),beforeDom=domState();
          const coherent=!options.force&&matches(view,before,beforeDom);
          const requestId=++requestSerial;
          if(coherent){
            skippedDuplicateCount++;
            lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId,reason:String(reason),committed:false,coherent:true,signature:view.signature,canonicalRevision:view.canonicalRevision,turnSerial:view.turnSerial,actorUid:view.projection.currentActorUid||null,dom:beforeDom,at:Date.now()});

            return lastRequest;
          }
          ActionRenderEngine.render(view,reason);
          commitSerial++;
          const after=ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),afterDom=domState();
          const converged=matches(view,after,afterDom);
          lastRequest=Object.freeze({requestId,reason:String(reason),committed:true,coherent:converged,signature:view.signature,canonicalRevision:view.canonicalRevision,turnSerial:view.turnSerial,actorUid:view.projection.currentActorUid||null,dom:afterDom,at:Date.now()});

          return lastRequest;
        };
        return Object.freeze({
          request,suspend,
          matches,
          reset:(reason="session-boundary")=>{
            requestSerial=0; commitSerial=0; skippedDuplicateCount=0; lastRequest=null;
            ActionRenderEngine.reset(reason);
            return Object.freeze({reason:String(reason),requestSerial,commitSerial,lastRequest:null,render:ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom:{...domState()}});
          },
          snapshot:()=>({requestSerial,commitSerial,skippedDuplicateCount,lastRequest:lastRequest?{...lastRequest,dom:{...lastRequest.dom}}:null,render:ActionRenderEngine.snapshot(),dom:{...domState()}}),
        });
      })();

      function openTimeAccelerationGather(a,t,s,u) {

      function submitManualSummonAction(a){
        const cur=normalizePendingSummonTurns(game); if(!cur||!a)return;
        const {q,u,owner}=cur;
        if(a.actionId&&a.actionId!==q.actionId)return;
        if(a.transactionId&&a.transactionId!==q.transactionId)return;
        if(Number.isFinite(Number(a.authoritativeRevision))&&Number(a.authoritativeRevision)!==Number(game.networkRevision||0))return;
        const canonicalAction={...a,actionId:q.actionId,transactionId:q.transactionId,sourceInstanceId:u.uid};
        if(game.mode==="online"&&p2p?.role==="guest"&&q.team===onlineSide&&!p2p.applyingRemoteAction){
          if(onlinePendingCommand||q.submitState!=="AWAITING_INPUT")return;
          q.submitState="SUBMITTED";
          q.submittedAction={type:canonicalAction.type||null,skill:canonicalAction.skill||null,targetUid:canonicalAction.targetUid||null,paymentPlan:canonicalAction.paymentPlan?{...canonicalAction.paymentPlan}:null};
          beginOnlinePendingCommand({
            type:"actionRequest",protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION,mode:"PVP",commandKind:"summon",
            matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),actionId:q.actionId,transactionId:q.transactionId,
            expectedTurnSerial:game.turnSerial,expectedTurn:game.turn,team:q.team,summonUid:u.uid,ownerUid:owner.uid,
            action:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(canonicalAction))
          });
          return;
        }
        q.submitState="COMMITTING";
        if(applyManualSummonAction(game,q.team,u.uid,canonicalAction))afterAction();
        else {q.submitState="AWAITING_INPUT";renderActions("manual-summon-rejected");}
      }
      function openSummonGatherChoice(base){

      function reconcileOnlinePendingCommand(msg) {
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (!pending) return Object.freeze({ settled:false, status:"none" });
        const actionId = String(pending.packet?.actionId || "");
        const resolution = msg?.actionResolution || null;
        let status = "pending";
        if (actionId && String(msg?.ackActionId || "") === actionId) status = "accepted";
        else if (actionId && String(resolution?.actionId || "") === actionId && ["accepted","rejected","unknown"].includes(String(resolution?.status || ""))) status = String(resolution.status);
        else if (Number(msg?.turnSerial) !== Number(pending.packet?.expectedTurnSerial)) status = "superseded";
        if (status === "pending") return Object.freeze({ settled:false, status, actionId });
        stopOnlinePendingCommand({ unlock:false, resolution:`authority:${status}` });
        busy = false;
        return Object.freeze({ settled:true, status, actionId });
      }
      function sendOnlinePendingCommand() {
        if (!onlinePendingCommand || !p2p?.connected) return;
        const pending = onlinePendingCommand;
        if (pending.suspendedAt) return;
        pending.attempts++;
        pending.lastSentAt = Date.now();
        if (pending.acked) {
          onlineSafeSend({
            type:"stateRequest", protocol:ONLINE_TURN_SYNC_VERSION, mode:pending.packet.mode||"PVP",
            matchId:pending.packet.matchId, actionId:pending.packet.actionId,
            knownTurnSerial:game?.turnSerial ?? -1,
            knownRevision:p2p.lastAuthoritativeRevision || 0
          });
        } else {
          onlineSafeSend(pending.packet);
        }
      }
      function beginOnlinePendingCommand(packet) {
        if (onlinePendingCommand) return false;
        onlinePendingCommand = { packet, attempts:0, acked:false, createdAt:Date.now(), lastSentAt:0, suspendedAt:0 };
        p2p.pendingActionId = packet.actionId;
        busy = true;
        renderActions();
        sendOnlinePendingCommand();
        if (onlineCommandRetryTimer && !window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(onlineCommandRetryTimer)) window.TimerManager.clear(onlineCommandRetryTimer);
        onlineCommandRetryTimer = window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setInterval?.(sendOnlinePendingCommand,650,"online-command-retry",{phase:"SESSION"}) || window.TimerManager.setInterval(sendOnlinePendingCommand,650);
        return true;
      }

      function timeMarkTimeline(g, type, payload = {}) {
        if (!g) return null;
        g.clockTimeline ||= [];
        const mark = {
          type,
          at: performance.now(),
          turnSerial: Number(g.turnSerial || 0),
          activeEntityId: g.activeEntityId || unitAt(g, ...(g.order?.[g.turn] || []))?.uid || null,
          ...payload,
        };
        g.clockTimeline.push(mark);
        if (g.clockTimeline.length > 160) g.clockTimeline.splice(0, g.clockTimeline.length - 160);
        return mark;
      }
      function timeCommitPendingTurnAdvance(g) {

        if (!pending) return false;
        delete g.pendingClockTurnAdvance;
        delete g._timeDeferTurnAdvance;
        const actor = findUnit(g, pending.actorEntityId) || unitAt(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex);
        g._timeCommittingTurnAdvance = true;
        try {
          if (actor) advanceDualOrEnd(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, actor, pending.usedSkill, pending.switched);
          else endTurn(g, pending.teamId, pending.slotIndex, pending.usedSkill);

        }

      function timeNormDeg(n) { n = Number(n) || 0; return ((n % 360) + 360) % 360; }
      function timeIsAligned(u) {
        if (!u || !cardHasCapability(u, "timeControl")) return false;

        return Math.abs(d) < .001 || Math.abs(d - 180) < .001;

bossHooks.scheduleAI = function(bossScheduleBefore){
      if(!game?.bossRaid)return bossScheduleBefore();
      if(game.winner||busy||game.pendingTraitChoices?.length)return;
      prepareCurrentTurn(game);
      const [t,s]=game.order[game.turn],u=unitAt(game,t,s),actions=legalActions(game,t,s),noLegalActions=actions.length===0;
      if(game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="guest"){busy=false;renderActions();return;}
      if(t==="P"&&!noLegalActions){renderActions();return;}
      const token=game.token,serial=game.turnSerial;
      busy=true;renderActions();
      let onlineTimerId=0;
      const run=()=>{
        if(game?.bossRaid?.online&&bossOnlineAiTimer!==onlineTimerId)return;
        bossOnlineAiTimer=0;
        if(!game||game.token!==token||game.turnSerial!==serial){busy=false;return;}
        if(noLegalActions){
          log(game,`${u?.name||"빈 슬롯"}: 가능한 행동이 없어 턴을 자동으로 넘김.`,"turn");
          endTurn(game,t,s,false);busy=false;afterAction();return;
        }
        const view=maskedStateFor(game,t),liveVisualEvents=visualEvents,liveVisualHoldMs=visualHoldMs;
        let a=null,reason="";
        try{visualEvents=[];visualHoldMs=0;a=u?.scheduledAction?{type:"scheduled"}:chooseAI(view,t,s,false);if(a&&!u?.scheduledAction)reason=aiReason(view,t,s,a)||"";}
        finally{visualEvents=liveVisualEvents;visualHoldMs=liveVisualHoldMs;}
        let ok=a?execute(game,t,s,a):false;
        if(a&&ok&&reason)log(game,`보스 AI 판단: ${reason}`,"aiwhy");
        if(!ok){const fallback=legalActions(game,t,s)[0];if(fallback)ok=execute(game,t,s,fallback);}
        if(!ok)endTurn(game,t,s,false);
        busy=false;afterAction();
      };
      const delay=Math.max(520,Number(document.getElementById("speed")?.value||900));
      if(game.bossRaid.online){if(bossOnlineAiTimer&&!window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.cancel?.(bossOnlineAiTimer))window.TimerManager.clear(bossOnlineAiTimer);onlineTimerId=window.OnlineRuntime?.sessionScope?.setTimeout?.(run,delay,"boss-online-ai",{phase:"SESSION"})||window.TimerManager.setTimeout(run,delay);bossOnlineAiTimer=onlineTimerId;}
      else scheduleLocalAutoTurnTimer(noLegalActions?"boss-no-legal-actions":"boss-ai",delay,run);
    };

    function bossSerialize(g=game){return serializeOnlineBattleProjection(g);}
    function bossRevive(raw){const g=JSON.parse(raw),rng=new RNG(g.seed);rng.s=g.rng?.s||rng.s;g.rng=rng;g.token=++battleToken;g.mode="bossOnline";bossSyncClock(g);return g;}
    function bossSendState(force=false,visuals=[]){
      if(!game?.bossRaid)return false;
      const packetVisuals=cloneOnlineVisualEvents(Array.isArray(visuals)?visuals:[]);
      const canHostSpectators=!game.bossRaid.online||(p2p.role==="host"&&p2p.connected);
      let sent=false;
      if(game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="host"&&p2p.connected){
        const ack=p2p?.__pendingAuthoritativeAck||null;
        if(p2p)p2p.__pendingAuthoritativeAck=null;
        sent=sendAuthoritativeOnlineState(packetVisuals,ack,force);
      }
      const spectator=window.__megaSpectatorR18;
      if(spectator&&canHostSpectators){
        const spectatorVisuals=packetVisuals.length?packetVisuals:cloneOnlineVisualEvents(visualEvents);
        if(spectatorVisuals.length)spectator.queueVisuals?.(spectatorVisuals);
        window.TimerManager.setTimeout(()=>{spectator.broadcastAnnounce?.();spectator.broadcastSnapshot?.(!!force);},0);
      }
      return sent;
    }
    function bossPassEmptySlot(g,t,s){
      if(!g?.bossRaid||unitAt(g,t,s))return false;
      endTurn(g,t,s,false);
      return true;
    }
bossHooks.afterAction = function(bossAfterBefore){
      if(!game?.bossRaid)return bossAfterBefore();
      pendingSkill=null;
      if(game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="guest"&&onlinePendingCommand){
        render();
        return;
      }
      const teammateVisuals=game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="host"
        ?cloneOnlineVisualEvents(visualEvents)
        :[];
      render();
      const reward=game.pendingTraitChoices?.[0];
      if(reward?.kind==="bossReward"&&reward.visualDelayMs==null)reward.visualDelayMs=true?Math.max(0,Number(visualHoldMs||0))+140:0;
      bossSendState(false,teammateVisuals);
      if(!processPendingTraitChoice())scheduleAI();
    };

    function bossHandlePacket(msg){
      if(!msg||typeof msg!=="object")return false;
      if(msg.type==="bossLobbyState"&&msg.protocol===BOSS_LOBBY_PROTOCOL){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||p2p?.battleStarted)return false;
        if(msg.matchId&&p2p.matchId&&msg.matchId!==p2p.matchId)return false;
        p2p.bossRemoteDeck=bossSanitizeDeck(msg.deck,2);
        p2p.bossRemotePlusStar=resolvePlusStarChoice(msg.plusStar,p2p.bossRemoteDeck,"A");
        p2p.bossRemoteReady=!!msg.ready&&bossDeckValid(p2p.bossRemoteDeck,2);
        showBossOnlineLobby();bossMaybeStartOnline();return true;
      }
      if(msg.type==="bossRewardOffer"&&p2p.role==="guest"){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||!game?.bossRaid?.online||msg.mode!=="BOSS_COOP"||String(msg.matchId||"")!==String(p2p.matchId||game.matchId||""))return false;
        if(game.pendingTraitChoices?.[0]?.serial!==msg.reward?.serial){game.pendingTraitChoices ||= [];game.pendingTraitChoices.unshift(msg.reward);}
        processPendingTraitChoice();return true;
      }
      if(msg.type==="bossRewardPick"&&p2p.role==="host"){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||!game?.bossRaid?.online||msg.mode!=="BOSS_COOP"||String(msg.matchId||"")!==String(p2p.matchId||game.matchId||""))return false;
        const q=game?.pendingTraitChoices?.[0];
        if(q?.kind==="bossReward"&&N
--- renderAction #15 offset=6632805 ---
rue;if(game.bossRaid.online&&p2p.role==="host"&&!q.sent){q.sent=true;p2pSend({type:"bossRewardOffer",mode:"BOSS_COOP",matchId:onlineCurrentMatchId(),reward:q});bossSendState(true);}return bossOpenReward(q);};

    function bossLocalControlsCurrent(g=game){if(!g?.bossRaid||g.winner)return false;const [t,s]=g.order[g.turn],u=unitAt(g,t,s);if(t!=="P")return false;if(!g.bossRaid.online)return true;return u?.bossOwner===p2p.role||(!u&&g.bossRaid.slotOwners?.[s]===p2p.role);}
    function renderBossActions(ctx=null){
      const viewModel=ctx?.viewModel||ActionPanelViewModelOwner.build(game),local=!!viewModel?.boss?.localControl;
      const r=viewModel.surfaceKind==="manual-summon"?renderManualSummonActions(viewModel):renderBaseActions(viewModel);
      const [t,s]=game.order[game.turn],current=unitAt(game,t,s);
      if(!game.winner&&!local)document.getElementById("actionTitle").textContent=t==="A"?"붉은 타임오러 계산 중…":"동료 행동 대기 중…";
      if(local&&t==="P"&&!current&&!game.winner){
        const pass=[...document.querySelectorAll("#actionButtons button")].find(button=>(button.textContent||"").includes("빈 슬롯 넘기기"));
        if(pass)pass.onclick=()=>{if(execute(game,t,s,{type:"bossPassEmpty"}))afterAction();};
      }
      if(game.winner){const box=document.getElementById("actionButtons");box.innerHTML='<button id="bossRaidAgain" data-action-panel-meta="terminal">보스전 메뉴</button><button data-nav="setup" data-action-panel-meta="terminal">메인 메뉴</button>';document.getElementById("bossRaidAgain").onclick=showBossRaidMenu;box.querySelector('[data-nav]').onclick=()=>NavigationApplication.request("setup");}
      return r;
    }
    ActionRenderEngine.registerRoute("boss-raid",300,()=>!!game?.bossRaid,ctx=>renderBossActions(ctx));

      if(!msg||typeof msg!=="object")return false;
      if(msg.type==="bossLobbyState"&&msg.protocol===BOSS_LOBBY_PROTOCOL){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||p2p?.battleStarted)return false;
        if(msg.matchId&&p2p.matchId&&msg.matchId!==p2p.matchId)return false;
        p2p.bossRemoteDeck=bossSanitizeDeck(msg.deck,2);
        p2p.bossRemotePlusStar=resolvePlusStarChoice(msg.plusStar,p2p.bossRemoteDeck,"A");
        p2p.bossRemoteReady=!!msg.ready&&bossDeckValid(p2p.bossRemoteDeck,2);
        showBossOnlineLobby();bossMaybeStartOnline();return true;
      }
      if(msg.type==="bossRewardOffer"&&p2p.role==="guest"){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||!game?.bossRaid?.online||msg.mode!=="BOSS_COOP"||String(msg.matchId||"")!==String(p2p.matchId||game.matchId||""))return false;
        if(game.pendingTraitChoices?.[0]?.serial!==msg.reward?.serial){game.pendingTraitChoices ||= [];game.pendingTraitChoices.unshift(msg.reward);}
        processPendingTraitChoice();return true;
      }
      if(msg.type==="bossRewardPick"&&p2p.role==="host"){
        if(p2p?.sessionMode!=="bossRaid"||!game?.bossRaid?.online||msg.mode!=="BOSS_COOP"||String(msg.matchId||"")!==String(p2p.matchId||game.matchId||""))return false;
        const q=game?.pendingTraitChoices?.[0];
        if(q?.kind==="bossReward"&&Number(q.serial)===Number(msg.serial))bossFinishReward(q,Number(msg.index),msg.pickerRole||"guest");
        else bossSendState(true);
        return true;
